TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了LLM机器翻译输出中的"翻译噪声"问题，定义了12种噪声模式并构建了TransClean基准，用于评估和提升翻译输出的干净度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在机器翻译中广泛使用，但输出常包含翻译以外的额外文本（如语言标签、解释、双语重复等），即"翻译噪声"。这一问题普遍存在，却缺乏专门的基准和系统性研究。

Method: 分析了12个LLM在22个语言对上的超79万条翻译输出，识别出12种反复出现的噪声模式，归为格式噪声和内容噪声两类。据此构建TransClean基准，包含9900对噪声-干净翻译输出（8800条合成、1100条人工整理真实样本），并评估两种抽取方法：基于译文质量估计模型的片段抽取法，以及提示LLM分离译文的LLM抽取法。

Result: TransClean基准涵盖大规模合成与真实样本，并对比了片段抽取与LLM抽取两种方法在去噪任务上的表现。

Conclusion: 该基准和分析为评估与改善LLM翻译输出的干净度提供了首个系统性框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for machine translation, yet their outputs often contain additional text beyond the translation itself, such as language labels, explanations or bilingual repetitions, which we term translation noise. Despite its prevalence, this problem lacks dedicated benchmarks and systematic study. We analyze over 790,000 translation outputs from 12 LLMs across 22 language pairs (LPs) and identify 12 recurring noise patterns, which we group into formatting and content noise. Building on the observed patterns, we construct TransClean, a controlled benchmark of 9,900 pairs of noisy and clean translation outputs, comprising 8,800 synthetically generated instances and 1,100 manually curated authentic instances. We evaluate two extraction approaches on the TransClean benchmark: 1) a span-based extraction method leveraging translation quality estimation models for span detection, and 2) an LLM-based extraction method that prompts an LLM to isolate the translation. Our benchmark and analysis provide the first systematic framework to evaluate and improve the cleanliness of LLM translation outputs.

</details>


### [38] [SWRouter: Similarity-Contractive Window Routing for Multi-Turn Large Language Model Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11414)
*Yu Wang,Yuchen Li,Rui Kong,Xinran Chen,Jiamin Chen,Hengyi Cai,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jiashu Zhao,Yulun Zhang,Zhonghao Lyu,Haoyi Xiong,Linghe Kong,Jimmy Xiangji Huang,Dawei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWRouter，一种用于多轮对话的大模型路由方法，结合相似度收缩窗口的上下文分段机制与双指标评估框架，在多轮对话基准上显著优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路由方法多针对单轮场景，直接迁移到多轮对话效果不佳，因为路由性能依赖历史上下文的分段、保留与整合方式。由此带来两大挑战：上下文构建中的信息丢失与信息混淆；以及在不混淆模型选择与提示构建质量的前提下评估路由质量。

Method: 提出SWRouter（相似度收缩窗口路由器），融合基于相似度的上下文分段机制用于提示构建，并设计双指标评估框架，将构建准确性与路由器性能解耦。

Result: 在多轮对话基准实验中，SWRouter持续超越强基线，相较最佳单个大模型评估准确率提升16.26%，相较Conv-ID Context基线再提升8.22%。

Conclusion: 多轮大模型路由需要上下文构建与评估的联合设计，而非对单轮路由方法的直接扩展。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit complementary strengths, motivating routing methods that dispatch each query to the most suitable model. Although existing routers are effective in single-turn settings, they do not directly transfer to multi-turn dialogue, where routing performance critically depends on how historical context is segmented, retained, and incorporated into the current prompt. This introduces two fundamental challenges: preventing information loss and information confusion during context construction, and evaluating routing quality without conflating model selection with prompt construction quality. In this paper, we propose SWRouter, a Similarity-Contractive Window Router for multi-turn large language model routing. SWRouter combines a similarity-based context segmentation mechanism for prompt construction with a dual-metric evaluation framework that decouples construction accuracy from router performance. Experiments on multi-turn dialogue benchmarks demonstrate that SWRouter consistently surpasses strong baselines, achieving a 16.26% improvement in evaluation accuracy over the best individual large language model and an additional 8.22% gain over the Conv-ID Context baseline. Our results highlight that multi-turn large language model routing requires a joint design of context construction and evaluation, rather than a direct extension of single-turn routing methods.

</details>


### [39] [Cross-Lingual Clinical Annotation Projection as Constrained Text Generation: A Six-Language Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11450)
*Álvaro Rey-Blanes,Francisco J. Moreno-Barea,Francisco J. Veredas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究将跨语言临床标注投影建模为"文本保持"的文档级生成任务：让LLM把实体标签直接插入不可变的目标语言文本，再通过确定性校验重构字符偏移。在将西班牙语疾病/症状/操作标注迁移到6种语言时，直接LLM投影效果最佳（GLM 5.2平均Strict F1=0.9201，本地可部署的Gemma4:31B达0.9133），全部18个语言-实体组合均超越此前SOTA（+0.0564~0.1512），共产出55,416个带偏移的实体提及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床NLP资源多集中于少数语言，标注数据稀缺限制了多语言临床语料构建。作者希望验证跨语言临床标注投影能否被形式化为一种可生成、可验证（字符级）的文本保持型生成任务，并厘清相对候选跨度投影流程的鲁棒性与计算代价权衡。

Method: 提出受约束的LLM投影工作流：将实体标签直接插入不可修改的目标语言文本，随后进行确定性校验与字符偏移重构。与监督式候选跨度投影、ML-LLM混合精炼两种方法对比；将西班牙语疾病、症状、操作标注迁移到6种语言。以MultiClinAI为金标准，采用严格跨度匹配与字符重叠F1评估；GLM 5.2与本地部署的Gemma4:31B均参与测试。

Result: 直接LLM投影表现最强且最稳定。GLM 5.2在18个语言-实体组合上平均Strict F1为0.9201，本地可部署的Gemma4:31B为0.9133。最优LLM配置在全部18个设置中均超越此前的SOTA，提升幅度0.0564~0.1512，最终产出55,416个带重构偏移的实体提及。

Conclusion: 直接基于LLM的投影可实现高质量的多语言临床标注迁移，为标注数据与语言特定工具匮乏的语言扩展临床NLP资源提供了实用方案；结合本地推理与确定性校验，可显著降低多语言临床语料构建的专家时间与成本。

Abstract: Background: To determine whether cross-lingual clinical annotation projection can be formulated as a text-preserving, document-level generative task that produces verifiable character-level annotations for multilingual clinical corpus construction, and to characterize its robustness and computational trade-offs relative to candidate-based projection pipelines. Methods: We developed a constrained LLM projection workflow that inserts entity tags directly into immutable target-language text, followed by deterministic validation and character-offset reconstruction. We evaluated it alongside supervised candidate-span projection and hybrid ML-LLM refinement for transferring Spanish Disease, Symptom, and Procedure annotations into six languages. Evaluation used MultiClinAI gold standard with strict span matching and character-overlap F1 Results: Direct LLM projection achieved the strongest and most consistent performance. GLM 5.2 obtained a mean Strict F1 of 0.9201 across 18 language-entity combinations, while locally deployable Gemma4:31B achieved 0.9133. The best LLM configuration improved Strict F1 over the previous state of the art in all 18 settings, by 0.0564-0.1512, yielding 55,416 grounded mentions with reconstructed offsets. Conclusions: Direct LLM-based projection enables high-quality multilingual clinical annotation transfer and provides a practical approach for extending clinical NLP resources to languages with fewer annotated datasets and language-specific tools. Combined with local inference and deterministic validation, it can substantially reduce expert time and cost for multilingual clinical corpus construction.

</details>


### [40] [ReGround: Grounding Reviewer Comments in Multimodal Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11460)
*Serwar Basch,Lizhen Qu,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReGround 数据集，将 10,267 条审稿意见关联到 3,656 篇论文中的 16,274 条证据，将"审稿意见定位"建模为检索任务，并揭示该任务的难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审稿意见通常针对论文的具体部分，但要在长篇多模态文档中把意见与其依据证据对应起来很困难。现有基准未覆盖这一场景，且多聚焦于显式的信息查询。

Method: 构建 ReGround 大规模数据集，利用作者rebuttal中常显式引用论文内容来回应审稿意见这一观察，作为高精度标注来源；将 grounding 建模为检索任务，并评测多种检索方法。

Result: 在整篇论文内容上检索表现较差；证据类型推断是主要瓶颈；多模态证据提供文本单独无法捕捉的补充信号。

Conclusion: ReGround 将审稿意见 grounding 暴露为科学文档理解中一个困难且具有实际重要性的问题。

Abstract: Reviewer comments naturally relate to specific parts of the reviewed paper, yet grounding these comments to the underlying evidence is difficult due to long multimodal documents. Existing benchmarks do not capture this setting and largely focus on explicit, information-seeking queries. We introduce ReGround, a large-scale dataset for reviewer comment grounding that links 10,267 reviewer comments to 16,274 evidence in the original anonymous submission of 3,656 papers. We build on a simple observation: author rebuttals often include explicit references to content of the submission used to address reviewer comments, providing a high-precision annotation source. We cast grounding as a retrieval task and evaluate a wide range of retrieval methods. Results show that retrieval over the entire paper content performs poorly, evidence-type inference is a major bottleneck, and multimodal evidence provides complementary signals that text alone misses. Our dataset exposes grounding reviewer comments as a difficult and practically important problem for scientific document understanding.

</details>


### [41] [Structural priors for data-efficient language learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11505)
*Yana Veitsman,Jonas Mayer Martins,Jonathan Lautenschlager,Lisa Beinborn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究用非语言数据预训练作为多语言建模的权重初始化，评估结构化迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效语言学习需要减少对大数据和算力的依赖，探索非语言数据作为语言建模先验的可能。

Method: 先在有结构的非语言数据（音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机等）上训练模型，再进行语言训练，通过下一词预测损失、权重变化和下游语言基准评估迁移。

Result: 音乐、概率文法、元胞自动机等符号数据类型带来比随机初始化更低的语言建模损失，且伴随更小的权重变化；但更低损失未稳定转化为更好的下游语言性能，迁移效率也低于直接用额外语言数据。

Conclusion: 非语言数据可作为下一词预测训练目标的部分替代，但不能可靠支持更广泛的语言泛化能力。

Abstract: Efficient language learning requires methods to reduce the reliance on large data and computational resources. We investigate structural transfer: First training models on non-language data to induce useful priors for natural language. This approach is a form of weight initialization for multilingual language modeling. We evaluate transfer via next-token-prediction loss, weight shifts in the model, and downstream linguistic benchmarks. Several symbolic data types - notably music, probabilistic grammars, and cellular automata - yield lower language-modeling loss than random initialization. These gains coincide with smaller weight shifts during subsequent language training, suggesting that structural transfer positions models in a more favorable region of the parameter space. However, a lower loss does not translate consistently into better downstream linguistic performance, and transfer from non-language data is less efficient than additional language data. We conclude that non-language data can serve as a partial substitute for language data for the training objective of next-token prediction but does not reliably support broader linguistic generalization.

</details>


### [42] [Complex-Text Robustness Evaluation and Failure Diagnosis for Low-Resource Multilingual Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11545)
*Tianlun Zuo,Ziyu Zhang,Tingzhi Mao,Zhonghua Fu,Lei Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出面向低资源多语言TTS的复杂文本鲁棒性诊断框架，从内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性三个维度评估泰语、越南语、斯瓦希里语和印尼语，并引入TRS文本风险分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低资源多语言TTS评估多聚焦于自然度、说话人相似度和常规短句的内容一致性，缺乏对复杂输入（数字、日期、命名实体、长句、语码转换、标点结构）下系统失败模式的诊断。

Method: 构建三（内容一致性、语言一致性、生成稳定性）维度诊断框架；针对泰、越、斯瓦希里、印尼语设计多语言鲁棒性测试方案，覆盖普通句和多种复杂文本；提出自动诊断指标（字符错误率、语种识别准确率、时长异常率）以及无需标注和训练的轻量级Text Risk Score（TRS）。

Result: 在OmniVoice、VoxCPM2、MMS-TTS三个系统上，复杂文本暴露了普通短句评估未反映的系统性失败模式；各系统在数字归一化、命名实体、长文本和语码转换上呈现不同脆弱点；TRS与内容错误和时长异常呈正相关。

Conclusion: 复杂文本输入揭示多语言TTS的系统性缺陷，TRS可作为低成本、生成前的风险诊断指标，用于低资源多语言TTS的复杂文本风险评估。

Abstract: Low-resource multilingual text-to-speech (TTS) systems have expanded language coverage, but their robustness under complex text inputs remains insufficiently diagnosed. Existing evaluations mainly focus on naturalness, speaker similarity, and content consistency using regular test sentences, while providing limited insight into how multilingual TTS systems fail when handling challenging inputs such as numbers, dates, named entities, long sentences, code-switched expressions, and punctuation-related structures. This paper proposes a complex-text robustness diagnosis framework for low-resource multilingual TTS. We evaluate robustness from three dimensions: content consistency, language consistency, and generation stability. A multilingual robustness testing scheme is designed for Thai, Vietnamese, Swahili, and Indonesian, covering ordinary sentences and multiple types of complex text inputs. We further introduce automatic diagnostic metrics, including character error rate, language identification accuracy, and duration abnormal rate. To support input-level risk analysis before speech generation, we propose a lightweight Text Risk Score (TRS), which estimates synthesis risk from interpretable text features without manual annotation or model training. Experiments on three representative multilingual TTS systems, including OmniVoice, VoxCPM2, and MMS-TTS, show that complex text inputs expose systematic failure patterns that are not fully reflected by ordinary short-sentence evaluation. Different systems exhibit distinct vulnerabilities in number normalization, named entity handling, long-text generation, and code-switched input processing. Furthermore, TRS shows a positive correlation with content errors and duration abnormalities, demonstrating its usefulness as a low-cost pre-synthesis indicator for complex-text risk diagnosis in low-resource multilingual TTS.

</details>


### [43] [A Training-Free, Alignment-Free Approach to Corporate Intelligence: Application to SEC Filings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11620)
*Jean-François Delpech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练、无需对齐的稀疏种子向量框架，将词串哈希到固定高维基，实现文档与时间跨度的统一坐标表示，用于高效的财务披露分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维稠密文本嵌入与大语言模型在财务披露分析中面临上下文窗口限制、幻觉风险、高计算成本，以及不同独立训练模型向量空间任意旋转导致无法对齐的问题。

Method: 将词串哈希到固定高维基，使所有文档和时间段天然处于同一坐标系；在句子上下文中累积种子向量，生成可线性组合的语料专属语义签名，支持文档比较、发行人指纹、词汇迁移追踪与主题句提取，且可在普通CPU上运行。

Result: 在多年SEC文件（10-K、10-Q、8-K）语料上，重大公司事件（如波音737 MAX危机、英特尔供应链中断、邦吉收购Viterra）呈现为独特且可解释的语义画像，并可追溯到产生该画像的原始句子，无需领域训练或LLM推理。

Conclusion: 该训练无关、对齐无关的框架为财务披露分析提供了低成本、可解释、可追溯的替代方案。

Abstract: High-dimensional dense text embeddings and large language models face real obstacles in financial-disclosure analysis: context-window limits, hallucination risk, high computational cost, and the arbitrary rotation of vector spaces across independently trained models. We present a training-free, alignment-free framework for corporate intelligence built on deterministic sparse seed vectors. Hashing word strings into a fixed high-dimensional basis places all documents and all temporal epochs in a common coordinate system by construction, removing any need for training or alignment. Accumulating these seed vectors across sentence contexts yields corpus-specific semantic signatures that compose linearly, supporting sub-second document comparison, issuer fingerprinting, tracking of how an issuer's vocabulary shifts between filings, and thematic sentence extraction, all on ordinary CPU hardware. Demonstrating the approach on a multi-year corpus of SEC filings (10-K, 10-Q, 8-K), we show how material corporate events, among them Boeing's 737 MAX crisis, Intel's supply-chain disruptions, and Bunge's acquisition of Viterra, emerge as distinct, interpretable semantic profiles, each traceable to the exact source sentences that produced it, with no domain-specific training and no LLM inference.

</details>


### [44] [Structured Transforms for Low-Overhead Quantization of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11687)
*Daria Cherniuk,Alexander Rudikov,Boris Kashin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Kashin分解的改进权重量化算法，用符号随机DCT替换稠密正交矩阵，降低每迭代成本至O(N log N)，并移除k-means多重重启瓶颈，实现稳定的2-bit聚类量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Kashin分解权重量化使用稠密随机正交矩阵，每迭代成本为O(N^2)，且依赖多重重启k-means来保证2-bit聚类稳定性，成为效率瓶颈。作者希望提升收敛性质、降低计算开销并保持数值稳定性。

Method: 保留将权重分解为两个分量（一个无穷范数有界，另一个经正交变换后无穷范数有界）的核心思想，将稠密随机正交矩阵替换为符号随机化的离散余弦变换（DCT），把每迭代复杂度从O(N^2)降到O(N log N)。采用带交替更新的贪心算法，保证各因子稳定2-bit聚类所需的四峰分布，并提供聚类中心的闭式初始化。结合OPTQ式顺序误差补偿与QuIP式非相干预处理，构建JAX流水线。

Result: 在OPT、Llama-2和Pythia上以每通道4-bit，与OPTQ、QuIP、QuIP-RG及免微调、免向量量化的QuIP#变体相比具有竞争力，且墙钟时间扩展性良好。在压力配置下，QuIP变体发散到四位数困惑度（Pythia-6.9B）或因LDL回代NaN中止（Mistral-7B），而Kashin-DCT保持数值稳定并接近FP16基线。推理时每权重分解为每通道两个2-bit因子码，适合原生2-bit硬件。

Conclusion: Kashin-DCT是一种收敛性更强、结构高效且数值稳定的权重量化方法，在保持竞争性量化质量的同时具备更好的时间复杂度和硬件适配性。

Abstract: We revisit Kashin-decomposition-based weight quantization for large language models and propose an improved algorithm with stronger convergence properties and structured, efficient orthogonal transforms. The method retains the core factorization of each weight into two components -- one with bounded infinity norm and the other with bounded infinity norm after an orthogonal transformation -- but replaces the dense random orthogonal matrix with a sign-randomized Discrete Cosine Transform (DCT), reducing the per-iteration cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to $\mathcal{O}(N \log N)$. The proposed greedy algorithm with alternating updates guarantees the four-peak distribution required for stable 2-bit clustering of each factor and admits closed-form initialization of cluster centers, removing the multi-restart k-means bottleneck of prior work. Composed with OPTQ-style sequential error compensation and QuIP-style incoherence preprocessing, the resulting JAX pipeline is competitive with OPTQ, QuIP, QuIP-RG and a fine-tuning- and vector-quantization-free variant of QuIP# at 4-bit per channel on OPT, Llama-2 and Pythia, with favorable wall-clock scaling. The bounded-$\ell_\infty$ factorization is also notably robust: on stress configurations where QuIP variants diverge to four-digit perplexity (Pythia-6.9B) or abort with NaNs in LDL back-substitution (Mistral-7B), Kashin-DCT remains numerically stable and stays close to FP16 baseline. At inference time, each weight decomposes into two 2-bit factor codes per channel that are structurally suited to native-2-bit hardware.

</details>


### [45] [Negative Self-Distillation: Learning to Reason by Avoiding Flaws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11699)
*Rongcan Pei,Zhepei Wei,Shuyao Xu,Xinyu Zhu,Wei-Lin Chen,Yu Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Negative Self-Distillation (NSD)，通过让模型生成"负面教师"（如粗心推理者）并使分布远离该负面条件，从而优化LLM推理能力，避免OPSD抑制探索性行为的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD)虽让模型利用特权信息自我提升，但在复杂推理任务上会严重损害性能——因为强迫学生模仿基于特权信息、人为自信的推理轨迹，会抑制不确定性表达，惩罚探索与自我纠正行为。

Method: NSD不依赖真值答案或外部监督，让模型自身生成针对问题的负面条件（如扮演"粗心推理者"）作为负面教师，推动学生分布远离它。为解决缺陷推理token与基础语言token混淆的问题，设计了动态门控机制，自动识别并隔离推理关键token，使梯度更新只针对行为缺陷而不破坏语言先验。

Result: 实验表明，NSD持续优于OPSD及其他无标签、自举式强化学习（RL）基线。

Conclusion: 通过"远离缺陷推理"而非"模仿特权解"的范式，NSD在保留模型语言能力的同时有效提升复杂推理性能，为LLM自我改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a popular paradigm for large language model (LLM) self-improvement, allowing models to act as their own teachers by leveraging privileged information such as ground-truth solutions. However, recent findings indicate that OPSD can severely degrade the performance of LLMs on complex reasoning tasks: By forcing the student to imitate an artificially confident reasoning trace conditioned on privileged information, OPSD inadvertently suppresses expressions of uncertainty and penalizes the exploratory, self-corrective behaviors required to solve challenging problems. To address this, we introduce Negative Self-Distillation (NSD), a new framework that optimizes LLMs by diverging from flawed reasoning rather than imitating privileged solutions. Instead of relying on ground-truth answers or external supervision, NSD uses the model itself to generate a question-specific negative condition (eg, acting as a ``careless reasoner'') and pushes the student's distribution away from this self-generated negative teacher. Naively applying unlearning objectives to achieve this divergence is problematic, as flawed reasoning tokens are confounded with basic linguistic tokens; indiscriminately penalizing both risks catastrophically degrading the model's foundational language capabilities. We resolve this by designing a dynamic gating mechanism that automatically identifies and isolates reasoning-critical tokens, ensuring gradient updates target only behavioral flaws while preserving the model's linguistic priors. Empirically, NSD consistently outperforms OPSD and other label-free, self-bootstrapping reinforcement learning (RL) baselines.

</details>


### [46] [The Eloquence submission for Task 2 of the Interspeech 2026 MLC-SLM challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11724)
*Jordi Luque,Lorenzo Concina,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti,Filippo Vella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Eloquence团队参加Interspeech 2026第二届MLC-SLM挑战赛Task 2（21语言的MCQA），探索三种方法，最佳成绩为macro-accuracy 0.81（多模态ICL），均显著优于官方基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨21种语言的多选题问答（MCQA）任务，并克服模型的强标签偏置问题，提升多语言语音理解性能。

Method: 三种方法：(1) LoRA微调Voxtral-Mini-3B，结合跨语言数据增强、ASR转录增强和时间戳感知音频裁剪；(2) 对冻结的Voxtral-24B应用多模态上下文学习（ICL）；(3) 免训练的三层语音锚定记忆检索系统，融合声学身份、语义内容和知识图谱。

Result: 方法一在Phase 2得0.72 macro-accuracy；方法二得0.81（最佳）；方法三得0.68。三者均显著优于官方基线。

Conclusion: 多模态ICL能有效纠正标签偏置并取得最佳效果；LoRA微调与检索系统也表现良好，均超越官方基线。

Abstract: This paper details the Eloquence team's approach to Task 2 of the 2nd MLC-SLM challenge at Interspeech 2026, which involves multilingual Multiple-Choice Question Answering (MCQA) across 21 languages. Three approaches are explored. First, we fine-tune Voxtral-Mini-3B via LoRA with cross-lingual data augmentation, ASR transcript augmentation and timestamp-aware audio cropping, achieving 0.72 macro-accuracy on evaluation Phase 2. Second, we apply multimodal in-context learning (ICL) to the frozen Voxtral-24B model to correct a strong label bias, reaching 0.81, our best result. Third, a training-free retrieval system based on a three-layer voice-anchored memory combining acoustic identity, semantic content, and a knowledge graph achieves 0.68. All three systems substantially outperform the official baseline.

</details>


### [47] [LOCUS: Task-Aware Low-Rank Post-Training for Token-Efficient Language Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11739)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 LOCUS，通过选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间来压缩输出长度，在偏好对齐中降低推理成本，同时保持效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型服务成本与输出序列长度直接相关，但标准偏好对齐常使回复变长却不提升效用。作者研究后训练更新的参数化方式是否影响生成长度。

Method: LOCUS 在低秩子空间内进行后训练，选择任务感知的低秩适配子空间，在效用约束下最小化输出 token 成本；在该子空间内保留原生偏好目标，骨干网络冻结。

Result: 在 Anthropic HH-RLHF 偏好数据上，基于 Pythia-2.8B 与 Qwen2.5-3B 评估，对比全参数 DPO、DrDPO 及 SamPO。LOCUS 在 Pythia-2.8B 上最多减少 39.84% 续写长度，在 Qwen2.5-3B 上减少 14.87–17.58%，仅更新 0.24–0.28% 参数，内部偏好诊断无实质变化。

Conclusion: 通过低秩子空间参数化可在不明显损害偏好效用的前提下显著缩短生成长度，降低服务成本。

Abstract: Large language model serving costs scale directly with output sequence length, yet standard preference alignment often inflates response verbosity without improving utility. We study whether the parameterization of post-training updates affects generation length: low-rank subspaces alter sequence length without modifying the alignment loss. We present LOCUS, a method that selects a task-aware low-rank adaptation subspace to minimize output-token cost subject to a utility constraint. Within this subspace, post-training retains the native preference objective with a frozen backbone. Across Anthropic HH-RLHF dialogue preferences, we evaluate two $\sim$3B decoder backbones, Pythia-2.8B and Qwen2.5-3B, against protocol-matched full-parameter DPO and DrDPO branches and the released SamPO checkpoint. LOCUS reduces continuation length by up to 39.84\% on Pythia-2.8B and by 14.87--17.58\% on Qwen2.5-3B while updating only 0.24--0.28\% of model parameters, with no material change in the internal preference diagnostic.

</details>


### [48] [RAG-Safety-Bench: Reliable Evaluation of Retrieval-Augmented LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11758)
*Adithiyan Rajan Indira Saravanan,Kathleen C. Fraser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RAG-Safety-Bench基准，通过分离检索质量干扰，将RAG安全场景划分为四种条件，测量RAG对LLM安全性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG可提升可靠性、减少幻觉，但会带来意外的安全副作用（如对有害请求生成危险内容）。随着用户将企业知识库接入RAG系统，亟需厘清导致安全退化的机制。

Method: 构建RAG-Safety-Bench，消除检索器质量的混淆效应，将问题分为四种条件：非RAG、RAG含可直接回答有害请求的oracle文档、RAG含与有害请求相关但无具体答案的文档、RAG含随机安全文档，从而隔离不同因素的影响。在5个开源LLM上评估。

Result: 发现良性能力与不安全能力呈反比关系；有力证据表明基线安全护栏并不能保证RAG场景下的下游安全；并针对特定模型支持"即使良性文档也可能导致检索系统生成不安全内容"的先前发现。

Conclusion: RAG对LLM安全性存在显著且机制可分离的影响，基线安全对齐不足以保障RAG系统安全，需针对检索增强场景专门设计安全措施。

Abstract: Allowing large language models (LLMs) to retrieve information from a set of trusted documents can increase reliability and reduce hallucination. However, recent work has demonstrated that retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can have unintended side effects on the overall safety of the generated responses, when prompted for harmful or dangerous content. A clearer understanding of the mechanisms leading to this result is needed, as increasing numbers of end users turn to RAG to incorporate corporate documents and knowledge bases into LLM-based systems. We introduce RAG-Safety-Bench, a benchmark to measure the safety impact of RAG on LLM models. By removing the confounding effect of retriever quality, and cleanly separating the problem into four conditions -- non-RAG, RAG with an oracle document containing the answer to the harmful request, RAG with documents related to the harmful request but without the specific answer, and RAG with random, safe documents -- the benchmark isolates the impacts of different factors in the observed safety degradation. We report results across five open-source LLMs, showing an inverse relationship between benign and unsafe capability, strong evidence that baseline safety guardrails do not lead to downstream safety guarantees in the RAG case, and model-specific support for previous findings that even benign documents can lead to unsafe generation in retrieval-enabled systems.

</details>


### [49] [Component-Aware Differential Privacy for Federated Multilingual Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11762)
*Jordi Luque,Fernando López,Aleix Sant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出α-split两池分配方法，解决语音大模型中声学编码器与语言解码器更新范数失衡导致的分层DP剪枝预算崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层差分隐私（DP）剪枝按参数量分配剪枝预算以提升梯度保真度，但当语音LLM的声学编码器与语言解码器更新范数相差一个数量级时，该方案失效。单池分层方法出现"跨组件预算崩溃"，使WER严重偏离平坦全局剪枝或导致训练崩溃；范数失衡较轻时自适应单池方法部分恢复，说明崩溃严重程度与组件间范数比成正比。

Method: 在六种分层方法和三种语音LLM架构上实证诊断根因，然后提出α-split：一种双池分配方法，将编码器与LLM参数归一化到独立的池中，并证明其联合ℓ2敏感度与原始(ε,δ)-DP保证保持不变。α按架构校准。

Result: 在架构校准的α下，方法恢复了与平坦DP相当的WER效用，同时对编码器提供了4.47倍更严格的逐组件噪声保护以抵御基于说话人声纹的梯度反演攻击，而LLM噪声开销仅增加2.6%。

Conclusion: 分层DP剪枝在语音LLM中因组件范数失衡而失效，α-split双池分配能兼顾WER效用与更强的编码器隐私保护，且不损失原始DP保证。

Abstract: Per-layer differential privacy (DP) clipping improves gradient fidelity in federated learning by allocating per-matrix clipping budgets proportional to parameter count. We show that this recipe breaks for speech large language models (speech-LLMs), when the acoustic encoder and the language decoder differ by an order of magnitude in update norm. Single-pool per-layer methods suffer \emph{cross-component budget collapse}, dragging word error rate (WER) far from flat global clipping or collapsing training entirely. When the norm imbalance is milder, adaptive single-pool methods partially recover, confirming that collapse severity scales with the inter-component norm ratio. We empirically diagnose the root cause across six per-layer methods and three speech-LLM architectures. We then propose \emph{$α$-split}, a two-pool allocation that normalises encoder and LLM parameters into independent pools, and show that joint $\ell_2$ sensitivity and the original $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP guarantee are unchanged. At architecture-calibrated $α$, our method recovers WER utility compared to flat DP, while granting the encoder $4.47{\times}$ tighter per-component noise protection against speaker voice-based gradient-inversion attacks at only $+2.6\%$ LLM noise overhead.

</details>


### [50] [Recognizing Is Not Reversing: A Controlled Inversion Test of Fact-Preserving News Framing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11769)
*Yi Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出受控的"框架反转"测试，考察LLM能否在保持事实不变的前提下撤销已知的框架变换。结果显示，模型事实保真度高（约0.84），但反转能力极低（0.044–0.068），即使正确识别框架类型与方向，反转率也仅0.071。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架研究多关注生成、检测或改写文本是否更"中立"，却未直接检验模型能否在事实固定的条件下撤销一个已知的框架变换，即框架识别与框架反转是否等价尚不清楚。

Method: 基于三种既有框架语言实现（评价性词汇、施事实现、信息显著性），构建受控反转测试：60篇新闻文章×三种干预强度，得到540组原子事实保持不变且编辑可追溯的配对变体；测试Qwen、DeepSeek、Kimi三个模型。

Result: 各模型事实保持率接近0.84，而干预反转率仅0.044–0.068；即使框架类型与方向均被正确识别，汇总反转率也只有0.071。

Conclusion: 事实保真、框架识别与框架反转之间存在明显分离：识别文章如何被框架化，并不意味着能够撤销该框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to analyze and rewrite news, yet current framing studies mainly evaluate generation, detection, or whether rewritten text appears more neutral. They do not directly show whether a model can undo a known framing transformation while keeping the facts fixed. We introduce a controlled inversion test over three established textual realizations of framing: evaluative lexis, agency realization, and information salience. Across 60 news articles and three intervention strengths, this yields 540 paired variants with preserved atomic facts and recorded edits. Across Qwen, DeepSeek, and Kimi, factual preservation remains near 0.84, whereas intervention reversal is 0.044--0.068. Even when both framing type and direction are recognized correctly, pooled reversal reaches 0.071. These results reveal a clear separation between factual fidelity, framing recognition, and framing inversion: recognizing how an article is framed does not imply that the framing can be undone.

</details>


### [51] [The widening evaluation gap in medical large language model research 2023 to 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11770)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文分析了2023年1月至2026年6月间PubMed上14个临床领域的11,628篇LLM医学研究文献，发现临床证据严重滞后于模型迭代，评估滞后从1.33个季度扩大到6.08个季度，随机试验往往评估更陈旧的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型每隔几个季度就被新一代取代，而临床证据的产出需要数年。作者想探究：医学研究是否跟得上它所评估的系统迭代速度？即评估的及时性与研究严谨性之间是否存在矛盾。

Method: 检索PubMed 2023年1月至2026年6月共11,628条记录，覆盖14个临床领域。统计研究设计类型（随机、对照或前瞻性设计），计算"评估滞后"（从研究引用的最新模型发布到论文发表的时间），并用固定模型构成的反事实基准衡量模型迭代对漂移的抵消作用，同时比较不同研究设计所评估模型的年龄。

Result: 文献量增长45倍，但仅2.5%采用随机、对照或前瞻性设计。评估滞后从1.33个季度扩大到6.08个季度。向新模型的迁移仅抵消56%的漂移（95% CI 50–65）。随机试验评估的模型中位年龄比其他设计老4.6个季度（P=3×10⁻¹⁹）；但若所命名模型仍在开发中，各设计间无差异；62%的随机试验评估的是已停产的模型家族。

Conclusion: 严谨性与时效性存在张力，而这种张力主要源于模型选择（研究倾向于评估更成熟但陈旧的模型）而非研究时间线本身。医学研究未能跟上LLM的迭代速度。

Abstract: Large language models are superseded every few quarters; clinical evidence takes years. We asked whether medical research is keeping pace with the systems it evaluates. PubMed returned 11,628 records for January 2023 to June 2026 across fourteen clinical domains, growing 45-fold; 2.5% used a randomised, controlled or prospective design. Evaluation lag, from a study's newest named model release to its own publication, widened from 1.33 to 6.08 quarters. Because discontinued models age mechanically, we benchmarked this against a counterfactual holding model composition fixed: migration to newer systems offset only 56% of the drift (95% CI 50-65). Randomised trials evaluated models a median 4.6 quarters older than other designs (P = 3 x 10^-19), yet among studies naming a model still under development no design differed from any other; 62% of randomised trials evaluated a discontinued family. Rigour and currency are in tension, and that tension reflects model selection rather than research timelines.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Word Error Rate: A Switch Aware Evaluation of ASR and Audio Language Models on English Yoruba Code-Switched Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11786)
*Chibuzor Okocha,Christan Earl Grant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对11个现代语音系统在英语-约鲁巴语码切换语音上进行切换感知评测，发现整体WER掩盖了码切换行为，音频语言模型在切换局部指标上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR与音频语言模型在单语基准上错误率已很低，但在低资源、带变音符号语言的码切换语音上表现尚不清楚，需要更细致的评测方法。

Method: 构建2000句确定性评测集与统一评分流程，评测6个ASR模型和5个音频语言模型；除WER外，提出切换进入Token错误率(SETER)、窗口化切换点错误率、语种特定错误率和忽略变音符号的WER等诊断指标。

Result: WER最优系统与领先音频LM在WER上无统计差异，但后者在所有切换局部指标上显著更优；忠实系统中约鲁巴语Token识别崩溃（错误率约0.97），英语识别更好，错误集中于切换进入约鲁巴语处；部分生成式音频LM作为精确转写器失败，出现翻译、冗长和提示泄露且强烈依赖提示。

Conclusion: 总体WER会掩盖码切换行为，需采用切换感知的细粒度指标评测；作者发布清单、指标实现与评测脚本以支持非洲码切换语音的可复现基准。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems and audio language models (audio LMs) now report low error rates on monolingual benchmarks, but their behavior on code switched speech in low resource, diacritic rich languages remains poorly characterized. We present a switch aware evaluation of eleven modern systems (six ASR models and five audio LMs) on English Yoruba code-switched speech, using a deterministic 2000 utterance evaluation set and a shared scoring pipeline. Beyond word error rate (WER), we report switch localized diagnostics: a switch entry token error rate (SETER), windowed switch point error rates, language specific error rates, and a diacritic insensitive WER. Our central finding is that aggregate WER hides code switching behavior. The best system by WER (an ASR model) is statistically indistinguishable from a leading audio LM on WER, yet the audio LM is significantly better on every switch localized metric. Across faithful systems, Yoruba token recognition collapses (error 0.97 for almost all systems) while English tokens are recognized far better, and errors concentrate sharply at switches into Yoruba. Several generative audio LMs fail as exact transcribers, producing translation, verbosity, and prompt leakage that are strongly prompt dependent. We release manifests, metric implementations, and evaluation scripts to support reproducible, switch aware benchmarking for African code switched speech.

</details>


### [53] [Target leakage, not model class, explains reported accuracy in survey-based cardiovascular screening: a leakage-tiered audit of glass-box and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11838)
*Raad Bin Tareaf,Murad Al-Rajab,Samia Loucif,Samer Ellaham,Cedric Schmitz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 心血管筛查模型的高AUROC主要源于特征泄漏而非模型能力；剥除泄漏特征后所有模型性能收敛，透明模型成本更低且可修复公平与不确定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验国家健康调查训练的心血管模型近0.89的AUROC究竟是真实学习还是目标泄漏，以及表格基础模型是否改变结论、部署所需的多维属性能否同时成立。

Method: 在BRFSS 2022的442,067名受访者上，对十种分类器（线性、树集成、神经网络、glass-box与表格基础模型）按五个递减泄漏风险的特征层级评测心肌梗死预测，审计判别、校准、显式阈值下的公平性、保形覆盖、解释忠实度与推理成本，并将模型与阈值冻结后外推至2023年430,755人。

Result: 移除两个诊断后特征使所有模型损失0.049-0.051 AUROC，压缩到0.0045宽的区间；glass-box EBM在0.005裕度内不劣于所有替代模型且比最强基础模型快约104倍；同一阈值女性检出75.4%而男性89.0%；边际保形覆盖男性0.86、60岁以上0.82，Mondrian校准修复所有分层；冻结模型外推AUROC差异在0.002内。

Conclusion: 文献中的性能余量来自特征集而非学习器；透明性无成本且使公平修复与不确定性条件化可审计，约束在于评估实践而非模型容量。

Abstract: Cardiovascular screening models trained on national health surveys routinely report areas under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) near 0.89. We asked whether that accuracy reflects learning or target leakage, whether tabular foundation models change the answer, and whether the properties deployment requires survive joint examination. We benchmarked ten classifiers spanning linear, tree-ensemble, neural, glass-box, and tabular foundation classes for prevalent myocardial infarction in 442,067 respondents of the 2022 Behavioral Risk Factor Surveillance System across five feature tiers of decreasing leakage risk. Each was audited for discrimination, calibration, fairness at an explicit screening threshold, conformal coverage, explanation faithfulness, and inference cost, then applied -- models and thresholds frozen -- to 430,755 respondents of 2023. Removing two post-diagnostic features cost every model 0.049-0.051 AUROC, collapsing the field into a 0.0045-wide band. The glass-box explainable boosting machine was non-inferior to every alternative within a pre-specified 0.005 margin while scoring the cohort roughly 104 times faster than the strongest foundation model. One threshold detected 75.4% of women's infarctions against 89.0% of men's; editing the model's shape functions reduced the gap to 0.010. Marginal conformal prediction gave 0.86 coverage to men and 0.82 to adults over 60; Mondrian calibration repaired every stratum. Frozen models transported within 0.002 AUROC. Reported headroom in this literature is a property of the feature set, not the learner. Transparency cost nothing measurable and made fairness repair and uncertainty conditioning directly auditable. Evaluation practice, not model capacity, is the binding constraint.

</details>


### [54] [IndicTriMix: Developing Language Identification Datasets and Models for Tri-Language Code-Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11851)
*Pruthwik Mishra,Rudra Trivedi,Avi Patel,Ashok Urlana,Shrikant Malviya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将代码混合文本的语种识别建模为序列标注任务，微调MuRIL与XLM-RoBERTa在印地语、古吉拉特语和孟加拉语的三种数据配置上进行词级语种识别，并公开基准数据集与模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中代码混合文本普遍存在，用户在同一语句中频繁切换多种语言，准确识别代码混合词元的语言成为迫切需求。传统面向单语文本的语种识别模型不适合代码混合场景下的词级语种识别。

Method: 将任务形式化为序列标注问题，微调适合印度语言的上下文Transformer模型MuRIL和XLM-RoBERTa；在三种数据配置（印地语、古吉拉特语、孟加拉语）上评估，预测每个词元的语言标签；提出两种使用三种语言平行句生成代码混合数据的方法。

Result: 在三种语言配置上评估了系统对词元语言标签的预测效果，证明上下文嵌入对多语言社交媒体文本的词级语种识别有效；发布了带有手动标注测试集的基准数据。

Conclusion: 上下文嵌入模型能有效完成代码混合社交媒体文本的词级语种识别；作者公开了微调模型和基准数据以促进可复现性与未来研究。

Abstract: Language identification in code-mixed text, largely observed in social media, is highly essential when users frequently switch between multiple languages within a single utterance. Accurately identifying the languages of code-mixed tokens becomes an urgent necessity. Traditional language identification models, designed for monolingual text, are not well suited for token-level language identification in code-mixed settings. We formulate the task as a sequence labeling problem and fine-tune contextual transformer-based models MuRIL and XLM-RoBERTa best suited for Indian languages. We evaluate these systems on three different data configurations (Hindi, Gujarati, and Bengali) to predict language labels for individual tokens. We release a benchmark for language identification in code-mixed tokens with manually annotated test sets. We propose two approaches of code-mixed generation using parallel sentences of three languages. The trained models demonstrate the effectiveness of contextual embeddings for token-level language identification in multilingual social media text. For reproducibility and to facilitate future research, we publicly release our fine-tuned models.

</details>


### [55] [Epistemic orientation predicts legislative effectiveness among members of the US Congress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11865)
*Segun Aroyehun,Stephan Lewandowsky,David Garcia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究将证据导向语言分析从国会整体转向议员个体，用EMI分数衡量演讲与推文中证据vs直觉语言，发现意识形态极端者用更少证据语言、跨平台一致、且EMI与立法效能正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证据导向沟通是民主治理、问责与集体决策的基础。既有研究表明1970年代中期以来美国国会议场演讲中证据导向语言下降，但分析停留在国会会期层面，未探究议员个体间是否存在系统性差异及其与政治行为和立法效能的关系。

Method: 采用"证据减直觉"（EMI）分数，衡量国会议场演讲和Twitter帖子中证据导向相对直觉导向语言的占比；将EMI与议员层面的意识形态、制度职位、沟通情境变量及立法效能分数（LES）相链接进行分析。

Result: 意识形态越极端的议员在国会议场使用更少证据导向语言；EMI具有跨平台一致性（会场上更证据导向者在Twitter上也更证据导向，但Twitter整体EMI更低）；即使在控制意识形态及政治、制度、人口、议题和沟通量等大量变量后，议场EMI仍与个体立法效能正相关。

Conclusion: 证据导向语言不仅是国会话语的总体特征，也是议员个体立法沟通与效能的有意义属性。

Abstract: Truth and evidence-based communication provide important foundations for democratic governance, accountability, and collective decision-making. Prior work shows that evidence-oriented language in US congressional floor speeches has declined since the mid-1970s, alongside broader changes in legislative productivity and polarization. This study shifts the analysis from congressional sessions to individual members of Congress to examine whether epistemic orientation varies systematically across legislators and whether it relates to political behavior and legislative effectiveness. Using the Evidence-Minus-Intuition (EMI) score, we measure the relative prevalence of evidence-oriented versus intuition-oriented language in congressional floor speeches and Twitter posts. We link these measures to legislator-level data on ideology, institutional position, communication context, and Legislative Effectiveness Score (LES). The results show that more ideologically extreme members use less evidence-oriented language on the congressional floor. EMI also exhibits cross-platform consistency with members who use more evidence-oriented language in floor speeches also being more evidence-oriented on Twitter, although EMI is lower on Twitter overall. Finally, EMI in congressional speeches is positively associated with individual legislative effectiveness, even after accounting for ideology and extensive political, institutional, demographic, topical, and communication volume controls. These findings suggest that evidence-oriented language is not only an aggregate feature of congressional discourse but also a meaningful attribute of individual-level legislative communication and effectiveness.

</details>


### [56] [Augustinian BabyLM: What Ostensive Definition Can and Cannot Teach a Small Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11870)
*Lisa Bylinina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在小规模掩码语言模型中实现"以视觉指示定义词义"(St. Augustine 式)的初始化:视觉接地词元用图像区域派生的嵌入初始化。视觉初始化留下持续到训练结束的可测量印记,但在多数BabyLM基准上不可见;唯一零样本例外是物体属性知识(COMPS)。用定制Visual-Property Swap基准发现视觉播种在播种词上有持久优势,且可因果迁移。功能词与抽象词也获得强视觉种子并在掩码预测损失上受益,但现有基准无一能捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验St. Augustine的"指示性词学习"设想:词义来自视觉指示而非纯语料统计。作者追问视觉接地初始化是否在语言模型中留下可测量的影响,以及现有评估能否探测到这种影响。

Method: 训练小型掩码语言模型(DeBERTa),语料1000万词。训练前给视觉接地词元的嵌入由其所标注图像区域派生,其余词元随机初始化。用BabyLM基准做零样本评测(含COMPS物体属性知识);构建语料定制的Visual-Property Swap基准(测颜色、材料、大小、形状知识,带逐项训练频率和播种状态);并对原本未播种词做合成接地作为因果检验;还考察功能词与抽象词的种子保留及留出掩码预测损失。

Result: 视觉初始化留下持续到训练结束的印记;在多数BabyLM基准(抽象语法知识)上无性能影响。COMPS上播种在所有配置中均有益。定制Visual-Property Swap上,视觉播种模型有持久的、可跨种子复现的优势,且局限于被播种词。合成接地能把优势因果地转移到恰好那些词上。功能词和抽象词也获得强视觉种子并全程保留,留出掩码预测损失在这些词上均下降——但没有任何基准能测出这一点。

Conclusion: 视觉接地初始化对语言模型有真实且持久、甚至因果可验证的效果,但主要体现于词汇属性/语义层面而非抽象语法层面,且现有基准普遍无法探测到这些效果(尤其功能词与抽象词上的损失改善)。需要新的评估方法来捕捉这类影响,这是开放问题。

Abstract: A language model normally begins training with random word embeddings: whatever 'banana' means must be learned from training corpora. I implement St. Augustine's picture of word learning, meaning by ostension, for a small masked language model (DeBERTa) trained on 10M words: before training, visually grounded tokens receive embeddings derived from the image regions they label; other tokens start random. Visual initialization leaves a measurable imprint that lasts until the end of training. At the same time, the effect remains invisible under most BabyLM benchmarks, which probe abstract grammatical knowledge: visual initialization does not affect performance there. The only zero-shot exception is object-property knowledge (COMPS, Misra et al. 2023), where seeding helps in every configuration. To follow up on this result, I build a corpus-tailored version of the Visual-Property Swap benchmark (Lin et al., 2026), which tests color, material, size, and shape knowledge, with per-item training frequency and seeded status. Here, vision-seeded models have a persistent, seed- replicated advantage, confined to the seeded words. As a causal test, I show that synthetic grounding of previously unseeded words transfers the advantage to exactly those words. Function words and abstract vocabulary also receive strong visual seeds and retain them throughout training, and the training objective draws on them: held-out mask-prediction loss falls for these words in every seed. However, no benchmark I run registers this. What evaluation would pick this up remains an open question.

</details>


### [57] [Domain-Specific Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11878)
*Varun Teja Chundru,Debasmita Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出融合DeBERTa微调、MC Dropout不确定性量化与温度缩放校准的多信号幻觉检测流程，在HaluEval上F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977，并用DPO将生成器幻觉率从85.5%降至37.7%，同时揭示跨域迁移差需领域微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成流畅文本时会产生不忠实的主张（幻觉），需可靠检测。同时现有检测器跨域泛化能力不明，且检测与缓解生成器幻觉需结合。

Method: 多信号检测流程：微调DeBERTa-v3分类 + MC Dropout不确定性量化 + 温度缩放校准，用于响应级幻觉检测；再做上下文消融与学习曲线分析；用DPO微调Qwen2.5-0.5B生成器降低幻觉；在SciFact上做跨域评估并对比PubMedBERT。

Result: HaluEval上F1=0.915、AUROC=0.977；各任务F1：QA 0.97、摘要0.96、对话0.82；MC Dropout推理准确率93.2%；摘要去上下文F1降24%；25%训练数据达77%性能；DPO将幻觉率85.5%→37.7%（相对降55.9%）；SciFact跨域F1=0.52，PubMedBERT微调F1=0.63、AUROC=0.81。

Conclusion: 多信号检测流程有效且具真实蕴含推理能力，检测器可用于指导生成端DPO缓解幻觉；模型跨域迁移差，领域匹配预训练是最强适配策略，需领域微调。

Abstract: Large language models generate fluent text that can contain unfaithful claims -- a phenomenon known as hallucination. We present a multi-signal detection pipeline combining fine-tuned DeBERTa-v3 classification, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout uncertainty quantification, and temperature-scaled calibration for response-level hallucination detection. Evaluated on the HaluEval benchmark, our pipeline achieves F1=0.915 and AUROC=0.977 on general-domain tasks, with per-task F1 scores of 0.97 (QA), 0.96 (Summarization), and 0.82 (Dialogue). MC Dropout inference further improves accuracy to 93.2%. A context ablation study confirms the model performs genuine entailment reasoning rather than exploiting surface patterns, with summarization F1 dropping 24% when knowledge context is removed. Learning curve analysis reveals that 25% of training data captures 77% of full-data performance. Beyond detection, we apply Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to a Qwen2.5-0.5B generator, reducing its hallucination rate from 85.5% to 37.7% (55.9% relative reduction) as measured by our detector. Cross-domain evaluation on the SciFact biomedical benchmark shows that general-domain training transfers poorly (F1=0.52), motivating domain-specific fine-tuning. PubMedBERT fine-tuned on SciFact achieves F1=0.63 and AUROC=0.81, demonstrating that domain-matched pre-training is the strongest adaptation strategy. Code and models are available at https://github.com/varunteja99/hallucination-detection-nlp

</details>


### [58] [Nuha-Speech: Building General-Purpose Arabic Speech-LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11892)
*Yingzhi Wang,Reem Alhazzani,Muhammad Alqurishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Nuha-Speech，一个面向阿拉伯语语音大模型的综合基础设施，涵盖数据集构建、模型训练与系统评估，构建了超150万条训练样本的阿拉伯语语音问答语料库，并基于Qwen-Omni进行微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音大模型日益多语言化，阿拉伯语仍严重缺乏代表性，亟需专门的基础设施来训练和评估阿拉伯语语音大模型。

Method: 1) 构建大规模阿拉伯语语音问答（SQA）语料库，含150万+训练样本，覆盖多种核心语音任务用于指令微调；2) 基于不同规模的Qwen-Omni模型变体进行监督微调；3) 设计包含多样任务和定制指标的评估框架。

Result: 构建了超150万样本的阿拉伯语SQA训练语料，完成了基于多种规模Qwen-Omni的监督微调，并建立了多样化任务与定制指标的评估框架。

Conclusion: 在阿拉伯语语音资源有限的约束下，为阿拉伯语语音大模型建立了基础性基础设施。

Abstract: As Speech Large Language Models (speech-LLMs) become increasingly multilingual, Arabic remains significantly underrepresented, highlighting the need for dedicated infrastructure to train and evaluate Arabic speech-LLMs.
  To address this gap, we introduce Nuha-Speech, a comprehensive initiative to develop general-purpose Arabic speech-LLMs spanning dataset construction, model training, and systematic evaluation. Specifically, we constructed a large-scale Arabic Speech Question-Answering (SQA) corpus comprising over 1.5 million training samples to allow instruction tuning over a broad range of core speech tasks. Then, the corpus was used for supervised fine-tuning based on Qwen-Omni model variants at different scales. Finally, we designed an evaluation framework featuring diverse tasks and tailored metrics. Through this work, we aim to establish foundational infrastructures for Arabic Speech-LLMs under constraints imposed by limited Arabic speech resources.

</details>


### [59] [Distance generalization in transformers: why bother with positional encoding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11913)
*Daniel Henrik Nevermann,Claudius Gros*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究 Transformer 在固定上下文长度下、训练与推理间 token 距离变化时的"距离泛化"能力，构造两种延迟复制任务并探究位置编码、数据多样性与迁移学习的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多集中于"分布外长度泛化"（短到长上下文外推），而对固定上下文长度、仅改变 token 间距离的"距离泛化"研究不足，需要理解其底层机制。

Method: 构造两个合成"延迟复制"任务，均涉及源与召回之间有限距离，token 被完整或选择性复制；在训练中未见过的延迟上测试模型。考察三方面：(A) RoPE、ALiBi 等位置编码相对无位置编码（NoPE）是否提升距离分辨率；(B) 数据多样性（训练中见到的 token 距离数量）对性能的影响；(C) 距离迁移学习何时为正、何时为负。

Result: 论文未给出具体实验数值，主要通过系统性实验回答上述三个问题，发现深入理解其底层机制至关重要。

Conclusion: 对距离泛化底层机制的理解是提升模型性能的关键，位置编码方案、数据多样性和迁移学习均对距离泛化有重要影响。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution length generalization, namely to extrapolate a task from short to longer context, has been studied intensively for transformers. Here we focus on distance generalization, which probes performance when inter-token distances are changed between training and inference, while keeping a fixed context length. We construct two synthetic delay copy tasks, both involving finite distances between source and recall, where tokens are copied either fully or selectively, and test models on delays unseen during training. We address three questions: (A) Do positional encoding schemes such as RoPE and ALiBi improve distance resolution relative to no positional encoding (NoPE)? (B) How does data diversity, the number of inter-token distances seen in training, affect performance? (C) When is distance transfer learning positive or negative? We present a thorough investigation, finding that it is paramount to improve our understanding of the underlying mechanisms.

</details>

### [60] [Deep Learning of Robust Market Making under Regime-Switching Order Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11614)
*Felipe Moret,Fabrizio Lillo*

Main category: q-fin.TR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于分布式DQN的深度强化学习做市商（RLMM），在零智能限价订单簿中优于GLFT，并通过辅助信号和场景-赌博机机制解决非平稳订单流下的库存饱和回撤问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典做市策略（Avellaneda-Stoikov、GLFT）依赖订单流平稳性假设，而真实市场中订单流存在由算法执行元订单等引发的状态切换（regime），导致这些方法出现负收益。

Method: 提出Rainbow风格分布式DQN（C51）做市商RLMM，并在零智能限价订单簿中校准与测试；在状态中引入两个辅助信号——方向流偏差的贝叶斯在线变点滤波器与队列调整后的报价敞口失衡；最后用场景-赌博机步骤对低收益regime场景重新加权。

Result: 平稳场景下RLMM在整个观测风险-收益前沿上优于GLFT，对订单流不对称更稳健，但在持续方向性失衡下仍因库存饱和产生大回撤；加入两个辅助信号后恢复盈利能力，场景-赌博机步骤在随机持续性和相关方向压力下进一步提升表现。

Conclusion: 深度强化学习做市商在平稳条件下优于经典策略，并通过在线变点检测、队列失衡信号与场景重加权有效应对非平稳订单流，提升稳健性与盈利能力。

Abstract: Classical market-making strategies based on stochastic control, such as the Avellaneda-Stoikov and the Guéant-Lehalle-Fernandez-Tapia (GLFT) extension, provide closed-form quoting rules, but rest on assumptions that break down at realistic microstructure timescales. One of them is that order flow is stationary, while empirical evidence points to the existence of regimes, possibly associated with algorithmic execution of metaorders. In this case, existing methods provide negative PnL. In this paper, we develop a deep reinforcement-learning market maker (RLMM) - a Rainbow-style distributional DQN (C51) which is calibrated and tested in a zero-intelligence limit order book. We find that, in the stationary setting, RLMM outperforms GLFT across the entire observed risk-return frontier. The RLMM is more robust to flow asymmetry than GLFT, but, like any stationarily trained strategy, it still suffers large drawdowns from inventory saturation under persistent directional imbalance. Augmenting the state of RLMM with two auxiliary signals - a Bayesian online change-point filter over the directional flow bias and a queue-adjusted quote-exposure imbalance -restores profitability. A final scenario-bandit step that reweights low-return regime scenarios further improves performance under random-persistence and correlated-direction stress.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [61] [Governing AI Research Through Peer Review: A Mixed-Methods Study of the Longitudinal Effects of Ethics Flags Across Resubmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10740)
*Kento Nishi,Alec Laprevotte,Isaiah Bullock,Mfoniso Andrew*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究发现AI会议的伦理标记更多改变论文呈现方式而非研究议程，作者常删除审稿时的让步；建议要求重投时披露既往伦理标记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择性AI会议通过伦理标记和审查要求引导研究走向更安全负责的实践，但作者是否真正调整研究议程尚不清楚。

Method: 追踪被拒/撤回且带伦理标记的ICLR投稿至后续公开重投，对446个重投案例按审稿后变化进行五类定性编码；并手动阅读25个案例的审稿与反驳，访谈作者了解反驳过程与重投决策。

Result: 83%的案例中作者未解决被标记问题，或仅修改论文而未改变相关方法/流程；作者在审稿人会更新评估时做出让步，但被拒后若认为批评不构成改变研究的理由则撤回让步。

Conclusion: 作者更易改变发表内容而非研究或构建对象，审稿被视为"编辑过程"；建议政策改革，尤其是重投时披露既往伦理标记以延续问责。

Abstract: Selective AI conferences have recently begun enforcing ethics flags and related review requirements, with the goal being to steer research towards safer and more responsible practices before publication. But do these requirements actually steer research as intended? In this paper, we show that authors more often revise how projects are presented following ethics flags than redirect their underlying research agendas. We first study the longitudinal effects of ethics flags by following rejected and withdrawn ICLR submissions with ethics flags into later public resubmissions, tracking manuscript changes after the ICLR review ends, when the original reviewers no longer oversee the project. We qualitatively code these resubmissions into five categories based on what changed after review and find that in 83% of 446 cases, authors leave the flagged concern unaddressed or revise the paper without changing the implicated methods or procedures. Then, we ask: if authors rarely change the research in response to ethics flags, what do they change instead? To answer this, we manually read reviews and rebuttals from 25 cases and directly interview authors about their rebuttal processes and resubmission decisions. We find that authors often concede concerns during rebuttal when reviewers can update their assessments, but drop those concessions after rejection when they do not regard the criticism as a sound reason to change the research. Interview participants describe publication changes as separate from changes to research direction, calling review an "editorial process" that shapes "what stories get seen" and, in another case, saying peer reviews are "mostly to filter out papers." Authors more readily change what they publish than what they study or build; we therefore recommend policy changes, especially disclosure of prior ethics flags upon resubmission so accountability carries over.

</details>


### [62] [Designing Technology for Social Wellbeing in Built Environments: A Conceptual Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10779)
*Gul Sher Ali,Michail Giannakos,Monica Lillefjell,Sobah Abbas Petersen*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该章节针对城市建成环境中数字技术支持社会福祉缺乏统一设计指导的问题，基于实证研究综合出概念框架，包含社会福祉维度模型、数字技术贡献矩阵和层次三部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字技术被用于城市建成环境以支持社会维度，但缺乏统一的设计指导；且社会福祉薄弱会影响身心健康，说明面向社会福祉的技术也可作为健康促进与预防干预，因此需要填补这一研究空白。

Method: 通过对所选关于嵌入建成环境中用于社会福祉的技术的实证研究进行综合归纳，提出一个由三个核心组件构成的概念框架。

Result: 框架包括三部分：1）识别数字技术可着力点的社会福祉维度模型；2）区分数字技术可作出何种贡献的贡献矩阵；3）映射技术支持社会福祉范围的层次。

Conclusion: 该概念框架可帮助研究者、从业者和政策制定者设计和指导面向建成环境中社会福祉的数字技术干预。

Abstract: Digital technologies are deployed in urban built environments with the aim of supporting social dimensions. However, the research offers no unified guidance for such digital technology design. Additionally, evidence shows that week social wellbeing contributes to mental and physical health outcomes which suggests that the technology designed to strengthen social wellbeing could also function as a form of health promoting and preventive intervention. This chapter addresses this research gap by developing a conceptual framework that supports the design of digital technologies for social wellbeing in built environments. We propose a conceptual framework composed of three core components which are drawn on the synthesis of selected empirical studies on technologies embedded in built environments for social wellbeing. First, a social wellbeing dimensions model that identifies what digital technology could address. Second, a digital technology contribution matrix that distinguishes the types of contributions a digital technology could make. Third, levels that maps the scope at which technology could support social wellbeing. This conceptual framework could help researchers, practitioners and policymakers to design and guide digital technology interventions that target social wellbeing in the built environment.

</details>


### [63] [Alternative AI Philosophy: Daoism as Method for AI in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10842)
*Qin Xie*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出道家思想作为重新想象AI教育的替代哲学框架，通过分析"道性""修身""真人"等概念，回应AI时代现实、认识论目标与伦理行动等根本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI教育的哲学讨论主要由西方知识传统主导，缺乏多元哲学视角。作者认为需要引入非西方思想资源以丰富该领域的哲学基础。

Method: 采用哲学分析与经典道家文本解读方法，并与当代AI教育研究展开对话。

Result: 道家"道性""修身""真人"等概念可为AI时代的教育现实观、认识目标与伦理行动提供新见解。

Conclusion: 本文推动了AI与教育研究的哲学声音多元化，拓展了概念资源，并为AI时代比较教育哲学提供了真正多元化的基础。

Abstract: As artificial intelligence (AI) rapidly iterates and transforms teaching, learning, and knowledge production, philosophical reflection has become increasingly indispensable to educational debates that remain predominantly shaped by Western intellectual traditions. This article proposes Daoism as an alternative philosophical framework for reimagining AI in education. Through philosophical analysis and textual interpretation of classical Daoist sources, brought into dialogue with contemporary scholarship on AI in education, it examines how the Daoist concepts of "Dao nature," "self-cultivation," and the "Zhenren" address fundamental questions concerning reality, the epistemic aims of education, and ethical action in the AI-mediated era. In doing so, the article diversifies the philosophical voices shaping inquiry into AI and education, enriching the field's conceptual resources for grappling with the philosophical questions AI raises for education and offering a genuinely pluralistic foundation for comparative philosophy of education in the AI era.

</details>


### [64] [Following the Preference, Missing the Optimum: Compliance Without Optimization in AI Housing Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10856)
*Hsuan Lo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文审计AI租房推荐：以可验证真值为基准，发现模型满足约束近乎完美，但39%推荐被严格支配，揭示"合规但未优化"的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有审计只表明模型会因身份偏见引导租房者，但缺乏枚举清单来量化"遗漏合适选项"给用户带来的实际损失，即无法衡量推荐被其他可选项支配的程度。

Method: 构建150个纽约市合成租房场景，每场景从120个真实房源（含租金、卧室数、GTFS通勤时间）中计算满足约束的精确集合并求Pareto前沿。以"被严格支配"（同池中有更便宜、通勤更快且卧室不少）为主结果，对两家厂商三个模型共9,945次调用审计，并做场景内操纵实验分离偏好理解与优化能力。

Result: 约束合规近乎完美（违反率1.8%，随机基线66.6%），但39%推荐被严格支配，支配房源中位便宜900美元/月、近3.5分钟。改一句话可正确移动中位租金646美元/月（偏好被理解），但推荐仍比同屏最便宜五个合规房源高606美元/月；明确词典序指令无改善。差距随候选集增大而扩大，跨OpenAI与Anthropic模型复现误差在3美元内。

Conclusion: 将失败刻画为"合规但未优化"，提出支配率作为可部署诊断指标，并公开代码、提示词及逐次调用结果。

Abstract: Large language models are becoming the first point of contact for consumer search in domains where the stakes are material and the law is explicit. Existing audits show that models steer housing seekers by perceived identity, but none can say what a user loses when a recommender overlooks a suitable option, for want of an enumerated inventory to score omissions against. We audit AI housing recommendation against a verifiable ground truth. For each of 150 synthetic renter scenarios in New York City we build a pool of 120 real listings with known rent, bedrooms and GTFS-computed transit commute, compute the exact set satisfying the renter's stated constraints, and derive its Pareto frontier. The primary outcome assumes no utility function: a recommendation is strictly dominated if the same pool holds a listing cheaper, faster to commute from and no smaller in bedrooms. Across 9,945 calls to three models from two vendors, compliance is near-perfect (1.8% violation against a 66.6% random floor), yet 39.0% of recommendations are strictly dominated, and the dominating listing is a median 900 USD/month cheaper and 3.5 minutes closer. A within-scenario manipulation separates two capabilities usually conflated: changing one sentence moves median recommended rent by 646 USD/month in the correct direction, so preferences are honored, yet recommendations still sit 606 USD/month above the five cheapest qualifying listings on the same screen, and an unambiguous lexicographic instruction gives no improvement under equivalence testing against a pre-specified 50 USD/month bound. The gap widens with candidate-set size and replicates across OpenAI and Anthropic models to within 3 USD. We characterize the failure as compliance without optimization, propose dominance-rate instrumentation as a deployable diagnostic, and release all code, prompts and per-call results.

</details>


### [65] [Work, Wellbeing, and Choice: Empirical Lessons for AI Futures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11019)
*Stephanie C. Y. Chan,Adam Bales,Katherine L. Hermann,Iason Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文综述心理学、社会学与经济学文献，探讨带薪工作与幸福感的关系，并从失业者、退休者、彩票中奖者、经济依赖配偶等群体中提取影响非工作状态幸福感的三大中介因素，进而推导AI自动化情景下的政策启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的自动化引发担忧：当带薪就业不再必要或稀缺时，人类如何获得幸福感？工作既被视为幸福来源也被视为阻碍，因此需要实证基础来回答工作与幸福感的关系及AI经济转型的影响。

Method: 对OECD国家、中国、印度和海湾国家的心理学、社会学与经济学文献进行比较综述，覆盖失业者、退休者、彩票中奖者和经济依赖配偶等群体。

Result: 识别出三个中介工作状态与幸福感关系的关键因素：(1)能动性与选择（自愿或非自愿退出及长期能动性）；(2)工作潜在收益的替代来源（志愿服务、爱好或国家提供的就业）；(3)社会与系统背景（工作文化规范和社会保障网强度）。

Conclusion: 基于这三个因素推导出针对不同AI自动化情景的具体启示，将实证证据与具体政策考量相连接。

Abstract: Advances in AI-driven automation have raised questions about how humans might find wellbeing in a world where paid employment is less necessary or less available than before. Paid work has been variously characterized as both a contributor and an impediment to human wellbeing. What is already known about the relationship between paid work and wellbeing? What factors influence wellbeing among people who do not work---or who do not need to work? And how might these factors bear upon prospective AI-induced economic transformations? To help provide empirical grounding for these questions, we survey the psychological, sociological, and economic literature that investigates the relationship between wellbeing and work. We draw on evidence from multiple populations, including the unemployed, retirees, lottery winners, and financially dependent spouses. This comparative review draws from studies across OECD countries, China, India, and Gulf states. We identify three key factors that mediate the relationship between work status and wellbeing: (1) agency and choice---whether the exit from work is voluntary or involuntary, as well as long-term agency; (2) the availability of alternative sources of work's latent benefits---such as volunteering, hobbies, or state-provisioned employment; and (3) social and systemic context---including cultural norms around work and the robustness of social safety nets. We draw on these three factors to derive specific implications for different AI automation scenarios, connecting the empirical evidence to concrete policy considerations.

</details>


### [66] [From Digital Accountability to Accountable Digitality Through Needs-Aware Information Systems: The Case of Auditable Child-Welfare Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11125)
*Soheil Human*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文反转数字问责的研究视角，提出"可问责的数字性"概念，并以需求感知信息系统为中介机制，探讨数字化转型如何使既有的人类制度更具问责性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多关注数字系统自身如何变得透明可审计；本文反向提问：数字化转型如何让人类制度更负责任。儿童福利判决是硬核案例，需兼顾保护儿童、保密与司法独立，同时实现需求与差异的聚合学习。

Method: 概念性、设计导向分析：在儿童权利约束下分解并重组数字与法律问责，推导理论到设计的规范链条、依赖机制、启示与保障措施。案例用于诊断与例证，非实证验证。

Result: 推导出理论到设计的规范链条及条件性中介机制，阐明隐私保护、共创、需求感知的信息系统如何支撑制度自我认知与可审计的司法。

Conclusion: 推进信息系统责任与伦理研究，表明需求感知信息系统可作为制度问责的中介，支撑可审计的正义与制度自我认知。

Abstract: Digital accountability research asks how digital systems can, among other aims, be made transparent, explainable, auditable, contestable, and supportive of ongoing learning and improvement. This paper reverses the question: how can digital transformation make established human institutions more accountable? It theorizes this reversal as accountable digitality and specifies needs-aware information systems as the mediating mechanism. The hard and paradigmatic case is child-welfare judgment, where best-interest procedures must protect children, preserve confidentiality, and respect judicial independence while enabling aggregate learning about needs, reasons, exceptions, and disparities. The case is used diagnostically and illustratively to derive and examine the design logic, not as empirical evidence or validation. Conceptual design-oriented analysis decomposes and recombines digital and legal accountability under child-rights constraints, deriving a canonical theory-to-design chain, contingent mechanisms, implications, and safeguards. It advances IS responsibility and ethics research by showing how privacy-preserving, co-created, needs-aware information systems can support institutional self-knowledge and auditable justice.

</details>


### [67] [(Whose defaults?) Is artificial intelligence reorienting archaeological methods?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11198)
*Lorenzo Cardarelli,Roberto Ragno*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM是否正在收窄考古学的方法多样性。通过分析约11.9万篇考古摘要及开展LLM推荐实验，发现文献中方法多样性略有上升，但LLM推荐明显趋同，倾向于推荐2023年前的流行方法，提示LLM可能推动方法选择趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI和"vibe coding"正在改变考古学的计算研究方式，但其对学科方法多样性的影响尚缺乏研究，作者希望评估LLM是否会收窄考古学家使用的方法范围。

Method: 首先分析Scopus中约11.9万篇2010–2025年考古摘要，用本地运行的LLM识别其中的计算方法，归入25个大类（L2）和241个细粒度聚类（L3），并用贝叶斯Dirichlet-multinomial模型分析子学科方法构成；随后开展对照实验，让两个不同开源模型针对28个考古研究问题推荐方法，并提供新手、中级、专家三档提示。

Result: 文献分析发现2023年后方法构成有微小但可信的变化，但小于整个研究期内的既有波动，无单一技术显著变化，整体方法多样性上升而非下降；实验表明LLM推荐多样性远低于已发表文献，尤其缺乏方法指导时，且更偏好2023年前广泛使用的方法，其推荐结果更接近2023年后的文献。

Conclusion: 结果与"LLM推动方法选择趋同"一致，但研究无法确立因果关系。作者提出更广泛的问题：随着LLM更深入参与研究，考古学应如何保持方法多样性。

Abstract: Generative AI and the practice of "vibe coding" are changing how archaeologists carry out computational research, but their effects on the discipline's range of methods is still understudied. In this paper, we evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) are narrowing the variety of methods archaeologists use. We first analysed approximately 119,000 archaeology abstracts from Scopus, covering publications from 2010 to 2025. Using a locally run LLM, we identified the computational methods reported in each abstract and organised them into 25 broad categories (L2) and 241 finer clusters (L3). A Bayesian Dirichlet-multinomial model of method composition within sub-disciplines found a small but credible shift in method use after 2023. However, this shift was smaller than the variation already present across the full study period. No individual technique showed a significant change, and overall methodological diversity increased rather than declined. We then ran a controlled experiment to see whether LLMs recommend a narrower set of methods than archaeologists have used in practice. Two different open-weight models were asked to suggest methods for 28 archaeological research problems, with prompts providing three levels of methodological guidance: novice, intermediate, and expert. Recommendation diversity was much lower than in the published literature, particularly without methodological guidance. The models also tended to favour methods that were widely used before 2023, and their recommendations more closely resembled the post-2023 literature. Taken together, these results are consistent with LLMs pushing methodological choice towards convergence, although our study cannot establish a causal effect. They raise a broader question: how can archaeology retain methodological diversity as LLMs become more involved in research?

</details>


### [68] [SoulAuth: An Actor-native Identity Architecture and Rust Reference Implementation for Humans and Long-lived AI Actors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11258)
*Kun Yuan,Harold Wang,Echo Li,Egusi Gui,Kiki Hu,Lucas Luo,Magnus Hu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出"Actor原生身份"概念与SoulAuth参考实现，主张长期存活的AI Actor应拥有不被Account/Credential/Client等替代的ActorIdentity，实现人机同为第一类身份主体，并区分认证与授权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正从短暂的模型调用转向跨凭证、客户端、会话和运行时实例长期存活的"actor"，身份基础设施需回答核心问题：规范性的连续性边界应置于何处。

Method: 采用"哲学工程"方法，将关于主体性的概念分析转化为身份对象、不变量、生命周期语义、系统职责、实现边界与可检验的一致性证据；并构建开源Rust实现SoulAuth。

Result: 针对固定版本SoulAuth v0.1.0的评估显示，实现达成了核心边界：Human/AIActor第一类身份地位、Client/Actor分离、认证/授权分离；但在统一Credential建模与锚定ActorIdentity的历史归因方面仍存缺口。

Conclusion: 架构一致性为部分达成而非完全达成；ActorIdentity应作为长期存活主体的规范连续性边界，SoulAuth提供了可参考的开源实现路径。

Abstract: As AI systems move from transient model invocations toward long-lived actors that persist across credentials, clients, sessions, and runtime instances, identity infrastructure must answer a basic question: where should the canonical continuity boundary be placed? This paper introduces Actor-native Identity and presents SoulAuth, an open-source Rust reference implementation for Humans and long-lived AIActors. We argue that any subject that must persist under its own identity and remain independently attributable should have an ActorIdentity that is not replaced by an Account, Credential, Client, AuthSession, IdentityBinding, or runtime instance. SoulAuth therefore treats Humans and long-lived AIActors as first-class identity subjects while keeping authentication distinct from downstream authority. Methodologically, we use a Philosophical Engineering approach that translates conceptual analysis of subjecthood into identity objects, invariants, lifecycle semantics, system responsibilities, implementation boundaries, and inspectable conformance evidence. Evaluation against the fixed SoulAuth v0.1.0 artifact shows that the implementation realizes core boundaries including Human/AIActor first-class identity status, Client/Actor separation, and Authentication/Authority separation, while gaps remain in unified Credential modeling and historical attribution anchored to ActorIdentity. We therefore report partial, not full, architecture conformance.

</details>


### [69] [A global mobile network coverage raster product at 1km resolution, 1999--2030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11320)
*Till Koebe,Theophilus Aidoo,Ali El Chami,Ali Kanso,Akansh Maurya,Purushottam Sharma,Ingmar Weber,Ridhi Kashyap*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个全球一致的、次国家级移动网络覆盖记录：1999-2030年214个国家和地区的2G/3G/4G覆盖概率1km地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动信号覆盖决定了数字时代人们能否工作、学习、银行服务、就医和应对危机，但此前缺乏全球一致、次国家级的移动网络覆盖记录。

Method: 用三个独立模型生成：校准的机器学习模型、网络建设的技术经济模拟器、空间深度学习模型；三者按国家、技术及实测精度加权合并为单一最佳估计，并提供逐像素90%不确定性区间。模型基于现有地理空间数据，并用2409份经质量筛查的运营商报告覆盖图调优。

Result: 2021-2024年地图仅由近期地理空间数据预测；2025-2030年由人口和基础设施预测外推。在训练留出国家上，机器学习模型AUC达0.89-0.92。

Conclusion: 该数据集可支持绘制全球数字鸿沟、将连通性与家庭调查结果关联，以及人道主义和基础设施规划。

Abstract: Where a mobile signal is available shapes who can work, learn, bank, seek health care and respond to crises in the digital age, yet no globally consistent, sub-national record of mobile network coverage exists. We present such a record: annual 1km maps of the probability of 2G, 3G and 4G coverage for 214 countries and territories for the years 1999 to 2030. The maps are produced by three independent models: a calibrated machine-learning model, a techno-economic simulator of network build-out, and a spatial deep-learning model. The three estimates are then combined, per country and technology and in proportion to their measured accuracy, into a single best estimate with per-pixel 90% uncertainty bands; all four layers are released as part of the dataset. Because mobile roll-out closely follows a country's socio-economic conditions (population distribution, electrification, physical infrastructure), the models are grounded in existing geospatial data and tuned on 2,409 quality-screened operator-reported coverage maps, which are available up to 2020. For 2021--2024 the maps are predicted from recent geospatial data alone; for 2025--2030 they are extrapolated from demographic and infrastructure projections. On countries held out during training, the machine-learning model attains AUC 0.89--0.92. Baseline comparisons and the combined product's external validation are reported in Technical Validation. The dataset supports mapping the global digital divide, linking connectivity to household-survey outcomes, and humanitarian and infrastructure planning.

</details>


### [70] [Characterizing Bluesky Content Moderation Service: From Automation of Service to Landscape of Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11373)
*Pushpdeep Singh,Sayeh Jarollahi,Ayan Majumdar,Vabuk Pahari,Abhijnan Chakraborty,Krishna P. Gummadi,Ingmar Weber,Abhisek Dash*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文利用Bluesky透明公开的审核日志，对2025年1060万条审核标签进行首次大规模审计，揭示其审核机制、有效性与目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流社交平台的审核系统部署不透明，导致内容审核的实证研究受到根本性限制；去中心化平台公开透明的审核日志为独立审计提供了前所未有的机会。

Method: 分析Bluesky Moderation Service（BMS）2025年的1060万条审核标签，从三方面研究：(i)机制（自动化与人工监督程度），(ii)有效性（有害内容检测准确率），(iii)目的（识别出的危害类型）。方法包括人工标注研究和无监督聚类。

Result: BMS为人机协作系统：性内容和图像内容标签数秒内自动应用，而细微、高风险标签需数小时至数天的人工监督。人工标注显示BMS精确率高（0.837）但召回率低（0.222），标注者在随机样本中发现的有害内容为系统检出的4.5倍。聚类揭示的危害包括针对受保护群体的敌意言论及色情、图像内容的传播。

Conclusion: 本研究展示了已部署审核系统的实际运作情况，为设计更有效、更透明的审核系统提供了数据驱动的基础。

Abstract: Empirical research on content moderation is fundamentally constrained by the opaque deployment of moderation systems on major social media platforms. To this end, the recent emergence of decentralized platforms with transparent, public moderation logs presents an unprecedented opportunity for independent audits. In this work, we leverage this architectural transparency to conduct the first large-scale audit of the default moderation system on Bluesky, the Bluesky Moderation Service (BMS). Analyzing its 10.6M moderation labels from 2025, we investigate three foundational aspects: (i) its mechanism (the degree of automation versus human oversight), (ii) its efficacy (accuracy in detecting harms), and (iii) its purpose (the landscape of harms it identifies). Our findings reveal a human-AI collaborative system where labels for sexual and graphic content are applied automatically in seconds, while nuanced and high stakes labels require more human oversight, taking hours or days. Through a manual annotation study, we find the BMS operates with high precision (0.837), but struggles with low recall (0.222), with our annotators identifying 4.5$\times$ more harmful content than the moderation system in a random sample. Finally, unsupervised clustering of the most frequently applied labeled posts uncovers detected harms ranging from hostility in discourse toward protected groups to the spread of sexually explicit and other graphic content. Our work offers a look into the operational realities of a deployed moderation system, providing a concrete data-driven foundation for designing more effective and transparent moderation systems.

</details>


### [71] [Buyer Artificial Intelligence-Enabled Environmental Governance and Supplier Environmental Controversies: An Organizational Information Processing and Signaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11391)
*Yongchao Martin Ma,Xinya Guan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究全球供应链中，买方AI环境治理是否降低海外供应商的环境争议，基于组织信息处理与信号理论，用文本分析和面板数据验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球供应链中的环境争议给全球买方带来重大风险，需探究买方AI环境治理能否约束海外供应商的环境问题。

Method: 基于组织信息处理理论与信号理论，用文本分析衡量买方AI环境治理，采用2020-2024年41国2505家美国上市公司供应商的面板数据与多维固定效应模型。

Result: 供应商受买方AI环境治理影响与其次年环境争议负相关；在供应商所在国AI准备度和监管质量更高时，该负向关系更强。

Conclusion: 研究为AI驱动的可持续治理与可持续供应链风险管理提供贡献，表明买方AI环境治理可有效降低供应商环境争议，且受制度环境调节。

Abstract: Environmental controversies in global supply chains pose significant risks for global buyers. This study examines whether overseas suppliers' exposure to buyers' artificial intelligence (AI)-enabled environmental governance reduces supplier environmental controversies. Drawing on organizational information processing theory and signaling theory, we investigate how suppliers' exposure to AI-enabled governance influences their environmental controversies and the institutional contingencies under which this effect varies. Using text analysis to measure buyer AI-enabled environmental governance, we analyze panel data on 2,505 suppliers of U.S.-listed firms across 41 countries from 2020 to 2024 with multidimensional fixed-effects models. We find that suppliers' exposure to buyer AI-enabled environmental governance is negatively associated with supplier environmental controversies in the following year. This negative relationship is stronger in supplier countries with higher AI readiness and regulatory quality. The study contributes to research on AI-enabled sustainability governance and sustainable supply chain risk management.

</details>


### [72] [Who Bears the Risk When Generative AI Enters Transport? A Distributional Sociotechnical Audit of Algorithmic Equity, Synthetic-Data Validity, and Public Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11611)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"分布性社会技术审计"(DSA),将算法公平、合成数据有效性与公众态度异质性整合为一个经验流程,用于交通领域生成式AI治理,并用连续的"社会技术风险指数"替代分类式审批层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI正进入交通领域(出行建议、合成事故记录生成、政策决策支持),但现有治理框架缺乏交通专属的统计工具,无法度量异质人群间的分布性风险。

Method: DSA流程包含四部分:(1)对四个LLM家族进行5,760次人格控制查询,覆盖12个人口统计线索与4个交通议题,使用两个跨家族评判模型与Wasserstein-2公平离散指数(EDI);(2)用条件投影最大均值差异(cpMMD)检验三种FARS事故记录生成器;(3)对Pew ATP Wave 152(N=4,538)拟合贝叶斯有序logit模型;(4)将各信号合成为连续的社会技术风险指数。

Result: 拥堵定价建议的人格分布离散度最高(平均EDI=1.96,最高直接EDI=2.20);CART合成事故记录未通过所有条件检验(p<0.001),高斯copula虽通过边际检验但条件压力处于临界(p=0.105);公众对AI的态度因人口分层而异;分类审批层级在权重扰动下出现75%的分配翻转率。

Conclusion: 分布性审计与带敏感性报告的连续风险指数,比分类式审批层级更能为交通领域生成式AI治理提供可辩护的依据。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence is entering transportation through traveler-facing advisories, synthetic crash-record generation, and policy decision support. Existing governance frameworks lack transport-specific statistical tools to measure distributional risks across heterogeneous populations. We develop a Distributional Sociotechnical Audit (DSA) that integrates algorithmic equity, synthetic-data validity, and public-attitude heterogeneity into one empirical pipeline. The audit analyzes 5,760 persona-controlled queries to four LLM families across 12 demographic cues and four transport topics, uses two cross-family judges and a Wasserstein-2 Equity Dispersion Index, tests three FARS crash-record generators with conditional projected maximum mean discrepancy (cpMMD), fits a Bayesian ordered-logit model to Pew American Trends Panel Wave 152 (N = 4,538), and combines the signals into a continuous Sociotechnical Risk Index. Congestion-pricing advice has the highest persona-based dispersion (mean EDI = 1.96; highest direct EDI = 2.20). CART synthetic crash records fail all conditional tests (p < 0.001), while the Gaussian copula has borderline conditional stress (p = 0.105) despite passing marginal checks. Attitudes to AI vary across demographic strata. Distributional audits and continuous risk indices with sensitivity reporting offer a more defensible basis for transport GenAI governance than categorical approval tiers, which show a 75% assignment flip rate under weight perturbation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [73] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出概率焦点搜索(PFS)，以概率p进行焦点引导、以1-p扩展最小f节点，加速下界推进与FOCAL扩容，在N-Puzzle/TSP上可减少约90%以上节点扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界次优搜索希望在w倍最优解内降低搜索代价，但FS的确定性策略可能长时间不改变f_min，导致FOCAL长期收缩，有用节点被延迟纳入，成为搜索瓶颈。

Method: PFS以概率p执行FS的FOCAL引导选择，以概率1-p扩展OPEN中最小f节点，从而推进下界、扩大FOCAL；同时将同一调度机制迁移到动态势搜索得PDPS，并扩展出anytime版本APFS。

Result: 在N-Puzzle、Pancake Sorting、TSP上对比FS，并在GCTSP上评估anytime扩展，变化多个w与p。f_min长期停滞时收益最大，节点扩展可减少约90%以上；APFS在GCTSP anytime评测中优于所有对比算法；Pancake Sorting等有效推进时收益较小；PDPS表明机制可迁移到势引导，但受领域与界影响。

Conclusion: 概率因子在FOCAL准入成为搜索瓶颈时最有效，能显著降低有界次优搜索代价，并可迁移到势引导类算法。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS).
