What is needed instead are \textit{AI model management systems} that enable models trained by different developers, for different tasks, with different data, and under different objectives to be identified, reused, and even assembled to address future user tasks. Because AI model developers are generally unwilling to share their training data, such systems should operate without accessing the training data of model developers and, ideally, without accessing raw data of future users. This requirement poses a fundamental challenge: the functionality of a modern AI model may not be fully understood even by the developer who trained it. How, then, can a system identify which models are useful for a given user task, let alone assemble models developed independently for different purposes? At first glance, this objective may appear unattainable. It becomes possible, however, by upgrading the basic unit of management from a machine learning model to a \textit{learnware}. \textit{Learnware = Model + Specification}. The specification, whose assignment transforms a trained model into a learnware, is generated with the help of a machine learning process without disclosing the training data of the developer and has a theoretically established data-preservation property. The \textit{Learnware Dock System (LDS)} provides a path toward powerful AI model management systems. Because specifications are generated according to a published reference and are comparable across models, they can also serve as an AI model \textit{collaboration protocol} through which independently developed models, including intelligent agents, can collaborate.

</details>


### [219] [Why Does Post-Training Quantization Work?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11716)
*Yuxiang Chen,Michael Beyer,Jun Zhu,Jianfei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究训练后量化为何有效，识别出两种机制使量化LLM保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练后量化会为隐藏状态引入误差，按直觉应随深度累积并破坏预测；但预训练模型误差累积很慢，即使从未在量化噪声下训练也能保持下游性能。作者试图解释这一现象。

Method: 对比全精度与量化前向传播，分析层间误差交互及LM-head几何结构对token概率的影响，并在不同模型和量化设置下验证。

Result: 发现两种机制：(1) 每层新引入的误差倾向于抵消其继承的输入误差，部分抵消使差异缓慢增长，该反作用残差交互在预训练中形成，是减缓隐藏误差增长的主要因素；(2) LM-head几何结构优先保留高排名token的分数和概率，这些通常是模型最有信心的预测。

Conclusion: 这两种机制共同解释了为何经过多层传递的量化误差仅产生微小输出变化，且结论在多种模型与量化设置下得到验证。

Abstract: Post-training quantization compresses large language models (LLMs) by storing their weights at reduced precision, and each quantized weight introduces an error into the hidden states. Naively, these errors should accumulate with depth and corrupt next-token prediction; randomly initialized models accumulate these discrepancies rapidly, whereas quantized pretrained models accumulate much less hidden-state error and largely maintain downstream task performance, even though they were never trained with quantization noise. This raises the question we address: why does post-training quantization work? Comparing full-precision and quantized forward passes, we identify two mechanisms that characterize pretrained quantization robustness. First, the error a layer newly introduces tends to oppose the error it inherits from the layer's input. The two cancel partially such that the discrepancy between full-precision and quantized passes grows slowly. This counteracting residual interaction develops during pretraining. Our quantitative analysis identifies it as a major factor slowing hidden-error growth. Second, LM-head geometry preferentially preserves the scores and probabilities of high-ranked tokens, which typically represent the model's most confident predictions. Together, these mechanisms explain why quantization error that passes through numerous layers can still produce only small output changes, and we verify the findings across models and quantization settings.

</details>


### [220] [Predicting Privacy Leakage from Weight Spectral Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11780)
*Richard J. Preen,Jim Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索用重尾自正则化框架中廉价的谱指标（如stable rank、Log alpha-Norm）作为成员推理攻击(MIA)脆弱性的代理，避免训练昂贵影子模型，发现其与MIA泄漏的相关性超过泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的MIA需要训练计算昂贵的影子模型，使大规模隐私评估不切实际，因此需要低成本、可扩展的MIA脆弱性代理指标。

Method: 在图像和表格分类任务上评估多个WeightWatcher谱指标，考察其与MIA隐私泄漏的关系，并与传统泛化度量（泛化差距）进行对比。

Result: stable rank与整体MIA成功率呈强正相关；Log alpha-Norm在低假阳性区间与MIA脆弱性持续负相关；这些关联均强于泛化差距所得到的关联。

Conclusion: 神经网络谱可能包含传统过拟合度量未能完全捕捉的隐私泄漏信息，支持将谱分析作为可扩展隐私审计的有前景方向。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) are widely used to audit the privacy disclosure risk of machine learning models, however current state-of-the-art attacks require training computationally expensive shadow models, making large-scale privacy evaluation impractical. In this work, we investigate whether inexpensive spectral metrics derived from the heavy-tailed self-regularisation framework can serve as proxies for MIA vulnerability. We evaluate several WeightWatcher spectral metrics on image and tabular classification tasks and compare their relationship with MIA privacy leakage against conventional measures of generalisation. Across datasets, stable rank exhibits a strong positive correlation with overall MIA success, while Log alpha-Norm shows a consistent negative correlation with MIA vulnerability at the low false-positive regime. These associations are observed to be stronger than those obtained using the generalisation gap. The results indicate that neural network spectra may contain information about privacy leakage that is not fully captured by conventional measures of overfitting, motivating spectral analysis as a promising direction for scalable privacy auditing.

</details>


### [221] [Dynamic language model representations for multi-objective reaction optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11790)
*Joshua W. Sin,David Ming Segura,Bojana Ranković,Siu Lun Chau,Marius D. R. Lutz,Andrea Anelli,Ryan P. Burwood,Kurt Püntener,Maximilian J. Notheis,Raphael Bigler,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出从文本动态学习反应表征的方法，将反应条件的文本描述通过微调语言模型编码，并与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，嵌入多目标贝叶斯优化循环中。该方法在镍、钯催化的交叉偶联等体系中，比描述符库或独热编码用更少实验达到优化收敛，并在氰化反应和三目标不对称氢化中实现克级放大的高产率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学合成需在多目标（产率、选择性、安全性）下优化反应，而模型驱动方法的关键在于如何表征反应组分。现有方法要么化学信息不足（独热编码），要么难以跨化学性质不同的组分扩展（分子描述符）。对结构和功能多样的组分，尚不清楚共享表征应包含什么，且构建这种表征本身是需针对每个新反应系统重做的艰巨任务。

Method: 绕过人工构建共享表征这一步骤，改为从文本动态学习反应表征。用微调的语言模型编码反应条件的文本描述，并将其与高斯过程代理模型联合训练，在多目标贝叶斯优化循环内生成任务自适应表征。

Result: 在镍和钯催化的交叉偶联体系（顺序与并行实验模式）中，该方法比描述符库或独热编码用更少实验即达到优化收敛。前瞻性应用于跨配体齿合度与异相添加剂的钯催化氰化反应，以及跨越手性铱和钌催化剂家族的三目标不对称氢化，两轮高通量实验（192个反应，不到各自设计空间的3%）得到的条件可直接放大到克级，分离产率分别为94%和84%，后者对映体过量达99.6%。

Conclusion: 通过语言模型从文本动态学习任务自适应反应表征并与贝叶斯优化联合，可有效替代人工设计的共享表征，在多类反应中更高效地实现多目标优化，并成功前瞻性放大到克级合成。

Abstract: Optimising chemical reactions across multiple objectives, such as yield, selectivity, and safety, is central to chemical synthesis, and model-driven approaches depend critically on how reaction components are represented. Established featurisations are either chemically uninformative, as with one-hot encodings, or, as with molecular descriptors, do not readily extend across chemically distinct components. For structurally and functionally diverse components, it is therefore unclear what a shared representation should contain. Constructing such a representation is itself a challenging research undertaking that must be revisited for each new reaction system. Here we bypass this step by learning the reaction representation dynamically from text. Textual descriptions of reaction conditions are encoded by a fine-tuned language model trained jointly with Gaussian process surrogates, yielding task-adaptive representations within a multi-objective Bayesian optimisation loop. Across nickel- and palladium-catalysed cross-couplings in both sequential and parallel experimentation regimes, this approach reaches optimisation convergence in fewer experiments than descriptor libraries or one-hot encoding. Applied prospectively to a palladium-catalysed cyanation spanning mixed ligand denticity and heterogeneous additives, and to a three-objective asymmetric hydrogenation across chiral iridium and ruthenium catalyst families, two rounds of high-throughput experimentation (192 reactions, under 3% of each design space) delivered conditions translating directly to gram scale in 94% and 84% isolated yield, the latter at 99.6% enantiomeric excess.

</details>


### [222] [Thinking with Looped Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11801)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Chanhyuk Lee,Floor Eijkelboom,Nicholas M. Boffi,İsmail İlkan Ceylan,Jinwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"looped flows"，用局部去噪目标训练循环模型的递归更新，避免因仅反向传播少数步而难以训练早期更新，并在推理时通过对概率流速度积分来花更多计算解决更难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型在推理时靠反复更新隐状态来投入更多计算，但训练时通常只反向传播一步或几步，导致早期更新难以学会支持后续更新，训练困难。

Method: 用局部去噪目标训练递归：通过逐步降低噪声水平并共享噪声，在去噪目标间建立时间关联，促使模型学到能随时间传递有用计算的递归状态，即使梯度只覆盖少数更新。推理时将其形式化为对学习到的去噪器所参数化的概率流速度进行积分，并结合递归状态，从而用更细的时间网格提升计算量，且不同初始噪声样本可得到多个有效预测。

Result: 在六个推理基准（含两个多解基准）上整体超越此前最先进的循环模型，ARC-AGI-1 测试准确率 58.8%，ARC-AGI-2 为 12.2%。

Conclusion: 通过局部去噪目标替代长程反向传播来训练递归，并将推理视作概率流积分，looped flows 能在有限梯度步下学到可迁移的递归计算，并通过更多计算与多次采样应对更难问题，取得新的最优表现。

Abstract: Humans and machines often solve harder problems by spending more time on computation. In deep learning, looped models implement this idea during inference by recurrently updating a hidden state. In practice, however, their training backpropagates through only one or a few updates, making it hard to train early updates to support future ones. We propose looped flows, an approach that sidesteps this issue by training the recurrence with local denoising objectives. By imposing temporal association across denoising objectives through progressively decreasing noise levels and shared noise, the model is incentivized to learn recurrent states that transfer useful computation over time, even when gradients cover only a few updates. We then formulate inference as integrating the velocity of a probability flow parameterized by the learned denoiser, coupled with recurrent states. This allows solving harder problems by spending more computation through a finer temporal grid and enables multiple valid predictions from different initial noise samples. Across six reasoning benchmarks including two multi-solution benchmarks, looped flows outperform prior state-of-the-art looped models overall, achieving 58.8% test accuracy on ARC-AGI-1 and 12.2% on ARC-AGI-2.

</details>


### [223] [Model-Aware Schedules Improve Generation via Fiberwise Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11842)
*Luyi Jia,Boyan Zhang,Yilun Liu,Steffen Rulands*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于纤维最优传输的模型感知调度，按预测风险与系数路径动能分配时间，一致优于强基线，并发现归一化风险与调度形变具有经验普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散/流匹配调度通过系数路径上的动能目标解释强基线，但该方法与模型无关，忽略了预测误差，无法针对具体模型优化信号与噪声的混合。

Method: 在概率路径固定时刻与状态下，相容的信号/噪声分解构成仿射纤维；在纤维内以最优传输代价平均真实分解与预测器诱导分解，定义纤维预测风险。将其与系数路径动能结合，得到闭式最优时间分配；方法可推广到一般线性预测目标，并可由早期基线检查点估计风险剖面。

Result: 在DDPM与流匹配上跨预测目标、训练配置、风险估计检查点、数据集与架构评估，模型感知调度稳定优于强基线，CIFAR-10流匹配在16次函数评估下FID相对降低38.6%。各模型无关动能基线确定自身动能参考坐标后，不同设置独立训练模型的归一化纤维风险剖面高度对齐，训练所用调度形变也对齐，预训练检查点诊断将该普适性扩展至更大的条件潜扩散与2-RF模型。

Conclusion: 模型感知调度能统一解释并超越动能基线；冻结的解析分配模板在无需再估计风险或模型特定拟合的情况下保留大部分收益，显示出跨模型与设置的普适性与实用性。

Abstract: Diffusion and flow-matching schedules control the signal and noise coefficients that mix data and noise along affine probability paths. Minimizing a kinetic action defined on coefficient paths, motivated by optimal transport, helps explain strong baselines but remains model-agnostic and ignores prediction error. Here we introduce a model-aware schedule construction based on fiberwise optimal transport. At a fixed time and state on the probability path, compatible signal/noise decompositions form an affine fiber. We define a fiberwise prediction risk by averaging optimal-transport costs between the true and predictor-induced decompositions within these fibers. On a fixed coefficient curve, combining this risk with coefficient-path kinetic action yields a closed-form optimal time allocation. This construction extends to general linear prediction targets, and the risk profile can be estimated from an early baseline checkpoint. We evaluate DDPMs and flow matching across prediction targets, training configurations, risk-estimation checkpoints, datasets, and architectures. Our model-aware schedules consistently outperform strong baselines, including a 38.6% relative FID reduction for flow matching on CIFAR-10 at 16 function evaluations. Each model-agnostic kinetic baseline determines its own kinetic reference coordinate. In these coordinates, fiberwise-risk profiles from independently trained models in different settings align closely after normalization to unit area. The resulting schedule deformations used in training also align, suggesting empirical universality across the evaluated models and settings. Pretrained-checkpoint diagnostics extend this normalized-risk agreement to larger conditional latent diffusion and 2-RF models. A frozen analytic allocation template retains most of the model-aware improvement without further risk estimation or model-specific fitting.

</details>


### [224] [The Last AI Built by Humans: Toward Genuine Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11873)
*Yi Duan,Ying Liu,Zirui Tang,Haodong Chen,Jun Zhou,Yumou Liu,Bangrui Xu,Yukai Wu,Sidi Chen,Yuhan Zhou,Haoyu Wang,Xiaoyou Yu,Shaokun Han,Xuzhou Zhu,Le Zhou,Bolin Lu,Wei Zhou,Jiachen Liu,Nuozhou Fang,Jiaxin Tian,Ruoyu Chen,Yuxuan Li,Kai Zuo,Kaiyan Zhang,Jiantao Qiu,Conghui He,Guoliang Li,Bowen Zhou,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhoufutu Wen,Jihua Kang,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述递归自我改进（RSI），提出发展路线图与多场景需求分析，并连接研究与实际系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM存在能力改进受限的问题（通过HCI指标揭示），需要RSI将经验与反馈转化为持续的自我提升。

Method: 用Headroom-Closed Index（HCI）揭示LLM问题，提出RSI概念及五阶段发展路线图；跨场景（科学发现、具身智能、软件工程）分析RSI需求差异，并结合产业实践与初步实证证据。

Result: 提出RSI发展路线图（改进-执行自主、改进策略自主、经验获取自主、环境适应自主、递归元改进），揭示各场景的独特需求与不同发展速度，并识别实现真正RSI的关键挑战。

Conclusion: RSI研究需与实用系统紧密结合，真正实现RSI仍面临多重关键挑战。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) enables AI systems to turn experience and feedback into persistent changes that improve both their capabilities and the process of future improvement. We first use the Headroom-Closed Index (HCI) to reveal the problems of existing LLMs, then introduce the RSI concept and its development roadmap: from improvement-execution autonomy, improvement-strategy autonomy, experience-acquisition autonomy, and environment-adaptation autonomy, to recursive meta-improvement. Next we examine RSI across scenarios (e.g., scientific discovery, embodied intelligence, software engineering), highlighting their distinct requirements and development speeds. Drawing on diverse industry practices and preliminary empirical evidence, we connect RSI research with practical systems and identify key challenges to achieving genuine RSI.

</details>


### [225] [CoRA-NAS: Coarse Ranking and Anchor-Residual Refinement for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11884)
*Yifan Yang,Zhaoyan Wang,Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Jiaojiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoRA-NAS提出两阶段框架，结合静态排序先验与低成本学习曲线修正，在多搜索空间实现高秩相关性和强架构选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零成本代理虽能廉价排序架构，但可靠性在不同搜索空间间差异大，需要更稳健的跨空间排序方法。

Method: CoRA-NAS含两阶段：CoRA-Rank用等权对数秩共识聚合容量与初始化结构代理，并加无目标共识门；CoRA-Refine采样锚点、外推早期验证曲线，用ExtraTrees学习残差修正，成本仅为全训练的约1%，且不使用完全训练标签拟合排序器。

Result: 在NAS-Bench-201、101、TransNAS-Bench-101、NATS-SSS上平均Spearman为0.946、0.715、0.786、0.894，最差空间0.715为对比方法最高；NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100上选中架构达73.32%，接近最优73.37%；纯尺寸空间上修正弥补静态先验相对参数量的不足。

Conclusion: 该框架兼顾跨空间排序鲁棒性与低成本架构选择。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies rank architectures cheaply, but their reliability varies across search spaces. We introduce CoRA-NAS (COarse Ranking + Anchor-residual), a two-stage framework combining a static ranking prior with low-cost learning-curve refinement. CoRA-Rank aggregates capacity and structure-at-initialization proxies through an equal-weight log-rank consensus and a target-free consensus gate. CoRA-Refine samples anchors across this prior, extrapolates their early validation curves, and propagates a learned residual correction with an ExtraTrees model. The refinement uses approximately 1% of the cost of fully training the candidate set. Fully trained architecture-accuracy labels are not used to fit the ranker. One configuration is used across spaces, with space-specific architecture encodings. Across NAS-Bench-201, NAS-Bench-101, TransNAS-Bench-101, and NATS-SSS, CoRA-Refine achieves mean Spearman correlations of 0.946, 0.715, 0.786, and 0.894, respectively. Its worst-space correlation of 0.715 is the highest among the compared methods. On NAS-Bench-201/CIFAR-100, its selected architecture reaches 73.32% accuracy, near the reported ground-truth best of 73.37%. On the pure size space, refinement recovers the static prior's shortfall relative to parameter count, while remaining tied with the strongest capacity proxies within noise. The resulting framework combines cross-space ranking robustness with low-cost architecture selection.

</details>


### [226] [CausalArena: Benchmarking Causal Discovery in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11897)
*Zi-Rong Li,Si-Yang Liu,Tian-Zuo Wang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CausalArena，一个统一、可演化的因果发现基准，覆盖合成、语义操作、公式驱动SCM及真实数据，揭示不同SCM家族与评测协议下方法排名显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现评测高度依赖SCM，但现有研究在图族、机制和评测协议上差异巨大；因果发现基础模型(CDFMs)的出现使问题更复杂，性能可能源于预训练环境与测试SCM的重叠而非因果发现能力，固定合成基准结果难以解释。

Method: 构建CausalArena统一可演化基准：合成SCM提供结构与机制的可控广度；语义操作SCM提供可人工审计、语义有据的环境；公式驱动SCM在明确科学机制下测试发现；公开真实数据集作外部效度检验。统一协议下评测经典、神经及预训练方法。

Result: 实验显示不同SCM家族与协议下方法排名发生显著变化，某一基准上的强性能无法可靠迁移到其他基准。

Conclusion: 基准多样性与预训练-评测重叠是基础模型时代因果发现评测的核心挑战。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover causal structures from data and is fundamental to scientific reasoning and intervention-based decision making. Its evaluation relies heavily on structural causal models (SCMs), which specify a causal graph together with the mechanisms that generate data, yet existing studies differ substantially in graph families, mechanisms, and evaluation protocols. The emergence of causal discovery foundation models (CDFMs) further complicates evaluation: performance may reflect not only causal discovery ability, but also overlap between pretraining environments and test SCMs, making results on fixed synthetic benchmarks difficult to interpret. We introduce CausalArena, a unified and evolvable benchmark for causal discovery under a common protocol. Synthetic SCMs supply controlled breadth over structures and mechanisms; semantic operational SCMs provide human-auditable, semantically grounded environments beyond standard synthetic generators; and formula-grounded SCMs test discovery under explicit scientific mechanisms. Public real-world datasets provide an additional external-validity check. Experiments across classical, neural, and pretrained methods reveal substantial ranking shifts across SCM families and protocols, showing that strong performance in one benchmark regime does not reliably transfer to others. These results highlight benchmark diversity and pretraining--evaluation overlap as central challenges for evaluating causal discovery in the foundation model era.

</details>


### [227] [TART: A Modular Tool for Technique-Aware Audio-to-Tablature Guitar Transcription](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11904)
*Akshaj Gupta,Hwi Joo Park,Andrea Guzman,Shamak Gowda,Samhita Konduri,Jiachen Lian,Robin Netzorg,Gopala Anumanchipalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TART，一个模块化的四阶段音频到吉他六线谱（tablature）流水线，直接从吉他音频生成带指法和演奏技巧标注的六线谱，在零样本设置下于多个基准上超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动音乐转录（AMT）用于吉他时受三大挑战限制：现有系统难以捕捉滑音、推弦、打击音等表现技巧；常将音符分配到错误的弦-品组合；且多在干净录音上训练，泛化到真实噪声音频的能力有限。

Method: TART是一个模块化四阶段音频到六线谱流水线：(1) 音频到MIDI的转录模型；(2) 表现技巧分类器；(3) 音频条件化的T5编码器-解码器用于弦-品分配；(4) 自动六线谱生成器。

Result: 在GuitarSet、EGDB及两个增强基准Noisy GuitarSet和Noisy EGDB上零样本评估。四项基准平均：音频到MIDI F50达81.35%（超最佳基线6.67分），弦-品Tab F1达71.8%（超最佳基线8.5分），端到端Tab F1达54.08%。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，TART是首个直接从吉他音频生成同时含指法和表现技巧标注的六线谱的框架。

Abstract: Automatic Music Transcription (AMT) for guitar remains limited by three challenges: existing systems often fail to capture expressive techniques such as slides, bends, and percussive hits; they often assign notes to incorrect string-fret combinations; and they are typically trained on clean recordings, limiting their generalization to noisy real-world audio. To address these challenges, we propose TART, a modular four-stage audio-to-tablature pipeline consisting of (1) an audio-to-MIDI transcription model, (2) an expressive technique classifier, (3) an audio-conditioned T5 encoder-decoder for string-fret assignment, and (4) an automated tablature generator. We evaluate TART in a zero-shot setting on GuitarSet, EGDB, and two augmented benchmarks, Noisy GuitarSet and Noisy EGDB. Averaged across these four benchmarks, TART achieves 81.35% audio-to-MIDI F50 (+6.67 points over the best prior baseline), 71.8% string-fret Tab F1 (+8.5 points over the best prior baseline), and 54.08% end-to-end Tab F1. To our knowledge, TART is the first framework to generate guitar tablature with both fingering and expressive technique annotations directly from guitar audio.

</details>


### [228] [From Protocols to Evidence: Bounded Claims for AI in Service of the Common Good](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11910)
*Nitesh V. Chawla,Paulo Benanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文主张AI不仅带来治理难题，更能暴露制度的失灵（回应性、归属感、关怀与问责）。因此负责任AI需同时评估系统与其嵌入的制度断裂。作者提出"断裂测试"（rupture test），区分"证据受限部署"与"测量受限治理"，并以RISE AI架构（责任、包容、安全、赋能）在限定范围内做出有据可依的主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI既是治理问题，也是制度失效的"探测器"。部署AI即是对既有制度状况的干预，可能修复、加剧、替代或掩盖原有失灵。现有从原则到协议（EU AI Act、NIST AI RMF、ISO/IEC 42001等）虽已推进，但未回答这些协议实际确立了何种权力结构、哪些权力未被触及、以及测量应在何处停止。

Method: 以教皇利奥十四世《Magnifica Humanitas》关于尊严、技术权力与共同善的道德框架为基础，构建"断裂测试"，将制度基准与系统评估相连接；并区分"证据受限部署"（仅就实际评估内容作主张）与"测量受限治理"（记录有利证据也无法推翻的约束）；在此边界内提出RISE AI架构。

Result: 提出了断裂测试及RISE AI架构，为在明确边界内就责任（Responsibility）、包容（Inclusivity）、安全（Safety）、赋能（Empowerment）做出有据可依的主张提供了框架。

Conclusion: 负责任AI不仅需要更好的工程与制度修复，还需持续的道德与政治判断；同时必须明确测量的边界与不可被证据推翻的约束。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence does more than create a governance problem. It can also reveal where institutions have already failed to provide responsiveness, belonging, care, and accountability. Once deployed, AI becomes an intervention in those conditions. It can repair, compound, substitute for, or conceal the failures it encounters. Responsible AI must therefore evaluate both the system and the institutional rupture into which it is introduced. The move from principles to protocols is already underway. The EU AI Act, NIST AI RMF, ISO/IEC 42001, and assurance practices translate commitments into roles, requirements, records, oversight, and assessment. The harder questions are what these protocols actually establish, whose power they leave untouched, and where measurement must stop. Pope Leo XIV's Magnifica Humanitas provides a broader moral frame centered on dignity, technological power, and the common good. Drawing on that frame, we develop a rupture test that links institutional baselines to system evaluation. We distinguish evidence-bounded deployment, which limits claims to what has actually been evaluated, from measurement-bounded governance, which records constraints that favorable evidence cannot override. Within those limits, RISE AI provides an architecture for making bounded, evidence-based claims about Responsibility, Inclusivity, Safety, and Empowerment. Responsible AI requires better engineering, institutional repair, and continued moral and political judgment.

</details>


### [229] [Data Scarcity and Model Sparsity: Mixtures-of-Experts Overfit More to Repeated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11917)
*Atindra Jha,Margaret Li,Jure Leskovec,Percy Liang,Luke Zettlemoyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究者系统比较了数据重复对稠密模型与MoE模型的影响，发现MoE随稀疏度增加对重复数据退化更快，并分析了过拟合机制与缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类文本数据逐渐枯竭，重复训练数据已成常规做法。已有研究关注稠密Transformer的数据重复，但对当前主流的稀疏架构（如MoE）在高重复率下的表现缺乏探索，而其计算效率更高，这一问题尤为关键。

Method: 在单域与多域数据混合中变化重复率，并在80M到1B激活参数（8.5B总参数）的MoE设置下改变专家数量与粒度；同时测试dropout等正则化方法；分析路由与专家专业化等内部机制。

Result: MoE比稠密模型退化更快，且随稀疏度加剧，由总参数而非激活参数决定；80M稠密模型可重复8倍以上而几乎无退化，MoE在4倍时即开始受损，32倍后性能低于稠密模型；强掩码正则化使MoE在重复64倍以上仍优于稠密模型，但均无法匹敌全唯一数据；MoE路由在训练早期即稳定，专家专业化与过拟合相关。

Conclusion: 揭示了稀疏性与数据重复之间的不利交互，指出过拟合的核心机制及潜在缓解途径，并建议通过破坏记忆模式来减少参数过度专业化。

Abstract: As the supply of human-written text is exhausted, it has become standard practice to repeat language model training data. Prior work has studied data repetition for densely activated Transformers, but the effects of data repetition remains largely unexplored for recently dominant sparse architectures such as Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), despite their increased compute efficiency. We vary data repetition rates across single- and multi-domain data mixes, and across MoE settings, including expert count and granularity. We consistently find, for models ranging from 80M to 1B active (8.5B total) parameters, that MoEs degrade more rapidly under data repetition. This effect increases with sparsity, dictated by total rather than active parameters. While 80M dense models can repeat data over 8x with minimal degradation, MoEs instead begin to suffer at 4x, and deteriorate rapidly, ceding their performance benefits in all-unique data settings to underperform dense models after 32x. We experiment with existing regularization methods as a potential remedy. We find that some methods, such as dropout, can mitigate overfitting. In particular, with strong masking-based regularization, MoEs are able to outperform dense models even when data is repeated more than 64 times. However, no method fully matches the performance of all-unique training data. Finally, we analyze internal mechanisms correlated with MoE overfitting in high repetition regimes, and find that MoE routing universally stabilizes early in training, and that expert specialization correlates with overfitting to repeated data. In sum, our work addresses the adverse interactions between sparsity and data repetition: we present evidence for the core mechanisms of overfitting and its potential remediation, and suggest promising avenues for future methods to reduce over-specialization in model parameters by disrupting memorization patterns.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [Weighted Empirical Risk Minimization for Machine Learning under Long-Range Dependence: Exact Pathwise Rates and Learning-Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10767)
*Elina Moldavskaya*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文为长程依赖数据上、采用正则加权经验风险最小化训练的平滑参数模型，建立了几乎必然学习的精确理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长记忆数据下学习理论的渐近行为需要精确刻画，尤其是记忆参数、加权和损失结构对学习速率与轨迹的影响。

Method: 假设观测来自平稳高斯序列的固定有限窗，权重正则变化；利用Wiener混沌秩m刻画损失梯度，并在长记忆内部区域把有限滞后得分在重对数尺度上约化为单个加权Hermite混沌。

Result: 得到几乎必然Bahadur表示、学习参数的精确limsup律，以及m≥2时完整学习轨迹的函数型聚点集；多项式学习指数由记忆参数和混沌秩决定且对可行幂加权不变，而路径常数与聚点几何依赖权重；秩一情形下所有最优幂指数均为正。

Conclusion: 给出了长程依赖下加权ERM学习的精确几乎必然理论，并展示于时间序列预测与分类。

Abstract: We develop an exact almost-sure learning theory for smooth parametric models trained by regularly weighted empirical risk minimization on long-range dependent data. The training observations are generated from a fixed finite window of a stationary Gaussian sequence, and the sample weights are regularly varying. If the loss gradient at the population minimizer has Wiener-chaos rank $m$ and a nonzero low-frequency coefficient, then, in the long-memory interior regime, the finite-lag score reduces on the iterated-logarithm scale to a single weighted Hermite chaos. This yields an almost-sure Bahadur representation, an exact limsup law for the learned parameter, and, for $m\ge2$, the functional cluster set of the complete learning trajectory. The polynomial learning exponent is determined by the memory parameter and the chaos rank and is invariant under the admissible power weighting, whereas the sharp pathwise constant and cluster geometry depend on the weights. In the rank-one case, global optimization over the admissible power exponents shows that every optimizer is positive. Time-series prediction and classification examples illustrate the results.

</details>


### [231] [A Hilbert-Valued Functional Decomposition Framework for Explaining Time-Dependent Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11295)
*Sophie Hanna Langbein,Niklas Koenen,Marvin N. Wright,Julia Herbinger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向时间依赖/多元输出的统一特征归因解释框架，将函数分解推广到Hilbert值预测函数，实现多时间粒度的特征解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于特征的归因方法主要针对标量输出，对函数型或多变量输出（如时间序列轨迹）只能逐点独立解释，忽略了输出各分量之间的依赖关系。

Method: 作者将函数分解（functional decomposition）推广到Hilbert值预测函数，并把已有的基于特征的解释框架扩展到该设定，引入基于核的输出表示，从而支持时间特定、时间分辨和时间聚合等多粒度解释，并给出统一视角，使现有方法成为特例。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据上验证了框架有效性，包括日内金融市场波动率预测与能源需求预测。

Conclusion: 该框架为时间依赖输出的特征解释提供了统一且灵活的工具，兼顾多时间粒度并涵盖现有方法作为特例。

Abstract: Feature-based explanations quantify features' influence on model predictions, but are primarily designed for scalar outputs. In many applications, however, outputs are functional or multivariate, such as time-dependent trajectories in demand forecasting. Consequently, existing approaches typically explain each output location independently, ignoring dependencies across the output components. We address this limitation by developing a unified framework for feature-based explanations of time-dependent outputs. Specifically, we generalize functional decomposition to Hilbert-valued prediction functions and extend an existing feature-based explanation framework to this setting. Our framework introduces kernel-based output representations that enable time-dependency-aware explanations at multiple levels of temporal granularity, including time-specific, time-resolved, and time-aggregated, while providing a unified view in which existing methods arise as special cases. We validate our framework on synthetic and real-world data, including intraday financial market volatility prediction and energy demand forecasting.

</details>


### [232] [Risk-Averse Decision Making with Multi-Level Reliability Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11524)
*Amirmohammad Farzaneh,Osvaldo Simeone*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究在系统状态不确定下，最大化多个目标中断水平性能证书的加权平均问题，将其转化为嵌套预测集优化问题，与共形预测相关联，并推导出可解耦的优化对偶形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程应用（如无线广播）需要针对不同目标中断水平提供性能证书，且系统真实状态存在不确定性，现有工作多局限于单级风险规避决策，无法处理多级证书需求。

Method: 将问题等价转化为对嵌套预测集的优化，连接共形预测文献并扩展单级风险规避决策方法；进一步推导对偶形式，实现跨输入值的优化解耦。

Result: 在基于分集的无线传输系统上进行数值实验，展示了用单一共享策略满足多级证书的代价，并刻画了多个可靠性水平之间的帕累托权衡。

Conclusion: 多级性能证书可通过嵌套预测集框架统一处理，对偶形式简化求解，实验验证多可靠性水平存在显著权衡与共享策略成本。

Abstract: Many applications in engineering, including wireless broadcasting, require designs that provide performance certificates at different target outage levels. This paper studies the problem of maximizing the weighted average of such certificates in the presence of uncertainty about the true system state. The problem is shown to be equivalent to an optimization over nested prediction sets, connecting to the literature on conformal prediction and extending prior art on single-level risk-averse decision making. Furthermore, we derive a dual formulation that decouples optimization across input values. Numerical experiments on a diversity-based wireless transmission system illustrate the cost of enforcing multi-level certificates with a single shared policy and trace the Pareto trade-off between multiple reliability levels.

</details>


### [233] [A distribution-free certification framework for trustworthy crash-severity prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11592)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一个无分布假设的认证层，可包裹任意碰撞严重度模型，提供序数集、逐类有效性、报告偏差迁移、分布漂移下及风险加权的有限样本保证，并用同一分析给出认证信息量的下界刻画；在520万德州记录上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞严重度模型用于筛查、调度与站点排序，却缺乏有限样本下"一次预测意味着什么"的说明。现有现成保证不适用：KABCO为序数结果；记录标签是现场评估，仅约一半与医疗严重度一致且误差结构化；部署还跨越校准未见的辖区与年份。

Method: 设计认证层，不改动基模型，利用上述结构：连续序数集（"B或更差"）；任意预设划分的逐类有效性及oracle效率刻画；通过声明的报告带将覆盖迁移到未观测真实严重度并给出最坏情况锐度；部署漂移下的单边证书；严重度加权风险控制。保证可随可归因松驰预算组合，并给出认证可实现性的信息量下界。

Result: 认证集信息量由真实分布的一个泛函支配，任何基模型都无法规避且无法无分布下界；给定从记录链接数据识别的误报通道，该下界可计算。在德州520万条、7个基模型、跨四十年数据上，认证层附着一致有效性；对弱势道路使用者给出模型无关的集合宽度下界，无基模型能突破，并将其与分布无关不可识别的余项分离。

Conclusion: 该框架为碰撞严重度预测提供了可部署的有限样本认证，明确了认证能做到与不能做到什么，并以带定理级测试的开源包发布。

Abstract: Crash-severity models inform screening, dispatch and site prioritization, yet are deployed without a finite-sample statement of what one prediction means. Off-the-shelf guarantees fail here, because the features that make crash severity distinctive defeat them: the KABCO outcome is ordinal, the recorded label is a field assessment agreeing with medical severity about half the time, erring in a structured way, and deployment crosses jurisdictions and years calibration never saw. We develop a certification layer that wraps any severity model unmodified, with distribution-free guarantees using this structure: contiguous ordinal sets that read as "B or worse"; per-class validity for any pre-declared partition, with an oracle efficiency characterization; transfer of coverage to unobserved true severity through a declared reporting band, with a worst-case sharpness result; a one-sided certificate under deployment shift; and severity-weighted risk control. The guarantees compose with an attributable slack budget. The same analysis bounds what certification can achieve. A certified set's informativeness is governed by a functional of the true law that no base model can evade and that cannot be lower-bounded distribution-free; given a declared misreporting channel identified from record-linkage data, a nonvacuous lower bound on that floor becomes computable. On 5.2 million Texas records across seven base models spanning four decades, the layer attaches identical validity and certifies, on the vulnerable road users, a model-independent floor on set width that no base model beats, separating it from a remainder that stays bounded but distribution-free unidentifiable. The framework is released as an open-source package with theorem-level tests.

</details>


### [234] [Identifiability of Nonnegative Tensor Decompositions via Positive Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11606)
*Haoming Wang,Ming Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文引入"positive scattering term"量化非负性带来的额外可辨识性信息，结合Lovitz–Petrov维度预算，给出非负张量分解的最小性与唯一性充分条件，并证明该散度项的代价仅为0、1或+∞（由图连通性刻画）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可辨识性由因子族的线性代数条件（维度与独立性）刻画，但对非负分解而言，正性提供了额外信息：非负项不能相互抵消，其支撑集约束了竞争性分解，而这些信息无法被维度与独立性条件单独捕捉。

Method: 构造positive scattering term并在中间因子空间上做优化，将其与Lovitz–Petrov版Kruskal定理的维度预算相结合；对每个分量子集S推导两条充分条件，核心是证明关于非负秩一张量不可约交换的positive splitting inequality，融合维度约束与支撑诱导的几何刚性。

Result: 阈值$2|S|-2$保证最小性与非负秩，更强的$2|S|-1$保证同长度非负分解中的唯一性；scattering term的模式代价恰为0、1或+∞，可用图连通性给出精确激活判据；该准则能严格认证超出Kruskal与Lovitz–Petrov条件的稀疏非负张量分解（含重塑后仍失败的反例）；矩阵情形下两准则分别退化为满秩分解与双边可分性。

Conclusion: 正性通过scattering term为可辨识性提供了独立于维度与独立性的新来源，所得到的阈值准则严格强于经典Kruskal/Lovitz–Petrov条件，并在矩阵情形自然退化为已知概念。

Abstract: Identifiability of tensor decompositions is often established through linear-algebraic conditions on the factor families. For nonnegative decompositions, however, positivity provides additional information that is not captured by dimension and independence alone: nonnegative terms cannot cancel, and their supports constrain competing decompositions. We introduce a positive scattering term that quantifies this additional source of identifiability and combine it with the dimension budget underlying the Lovitz--Petrov generalization of Kruskal's theorem. For every subset of components, we obtain two sufficient conditions: a threshold of $2|S|-2$ guarantees minimality and nonnegative rank, while the stronger threshold $2|S|-1$ guarantees uniqueness among nonnegative decompositions of the same length. The key result is a positive splitting inequality for irreducible exchanges of nonnegative rank-one tensors, which combines the dimension constraint with support-induced geometric rigidity. Although the scattering term is defined through an optimization over intermediate factor spaces, we show that its mode costs are exactly $0$, $1$, or $+\infty$, yielding an exact activation characterization in terms of graph connectivity. The resulting criterion can strictly certify sparse nonnegative tensor decompositions beyond the reach of Kruskal and Lovitz--Petrov conditions, including examples for which those conditions fail even after reshaping. In the matrix case, the two criteria reduce respectively to full-rank factorization and two-sided separability.

</details>


### [235] [Generalization Analysis of Distributed Kernel-based Robust Gradient Descent Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11712)
*Jun-Yi Meng,Zheng-Chu Guo,Yuan Mao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究再生核希尔伯特空间中分布式梯度下降在鲁棒损失下的泛化性能，建立最优学习率并提出通信高效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式核方法在鲁棒损失函数下的泛化性能尚不明确，尤其是饱和现象与统计鲁棒性之间的权衡，以及局部机器数量限制问题需要解决。

Method: 利用梯度下降的谱刻画与鲁棒损失函数的固有性质，为分布式核鲁棒梯度下降（DKRGD）算法选择合适的尺度参数σ，并进行新的误差分析以改进算子乘积界。

Result: 建立了DKRGD的最优学习率；σ的选择同时缓解饱和现象并保证统计鲁棒性；新误差分析显著放宽了对局部机器最大数量的限制，同时保持最优学习率；并开发了通信高效策略以进一步提升收敛性能。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布式核鲁棒梯度下降提供了理论保证与更宽松的条件，并通过通信高效策略改善了收敛表现。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the generalization performance of distributed gradient descent algorithms in a reproducing kernel Hilbert space under a robust loss function $l_σ$. By exploiting the spectral characterization of gradient descent together with the intrinsic properties of robust loss functions, we establish optimal learning rates for the distributed kernel-based robust gradient descent (DKRGD) algorithm with an appropriately chosen scale parameter $σ$. The proposed parameter choice of $σ$ simultaneously alleviates the saturation phenomenon and guarantees statistical robustness. A key technical contribution is a novel error analysis that provides substantially sharper bounds for products of operators, thereby significantly relaxing existing restrictions on the maximum number of local machines while retaining optimal learning rates. Finally, we develop a communication-efficient strategy that further improves the convergence performance of DKRGD.

</details>


### [236] [Near-Optimal Reinforcement Learning with Multi-Step Transition Lookahead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11807)
*Corentin Pla,Hugo Richard,Marc Abeille,Vianney Perchet*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究带转移前瞻（transition look-ahead）的强化学习：证明对任意固定有理折扣因子精确规划仍为NP难，并提出每步前瞻深度的随机多项式时间近似方案，进一步扩展到未知转移与随机奖励，达到与经典表格折扣RL匹配的累积遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前瞻能显著提升性能，但已知多步转移前瞻的最优规划是NP难的——然而该硬度结论仅用折扣因子趋近1建立，故此前不清楚：(1) 对任意折扣因子问题是否仍难；(2) 是否仍可高效地进行近最优规划。本文旨在同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 首先对每个固定有理折扣因子γ∈(0,1)构造精确规划的NP难性证明；随后为每个固定前瞻深度引入随机多项式时间近似方案（近似算法）。并利用乐观性与方差自适应置信界，将方法推广到未知转移与随机奖励的情形。

Result: (1) 对任意固定有理折扣因子，精确规划均为NP难；(2) 对每个固定前瞻深度，存在随机多项式时间近似方案；(3) 在未知转移与随机奖励下，所得算法的累积遗憾主项（leading term）在忽略对数因子的意义下与经典表格折扣RL相匹配。

Conclusion: 尽管带转移前瞻的精确规划是NP难的，但高效的近最优规划与学习仍然可行，从而在前瞻规划的可计算性与可学习性之间建立了清晰的分界。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning (RL) with transition look-ahead, where the agent may observe which states would be visited upon playing any sequence of $\ell$ actions before deciding its course of action. Although look-ahead can substantially improve achievable performance, it is known that optimal planning with multi-step transition look-ahead is NP-hard, but this hardness was established using discount factors arbitrarily close to one. It was therefore unknown whether the problem remains hard for any discount factor, and whether near-optimal planning can nevertheless be performed efficiently. We resolve both questions. First, we show that for every fixed rational discount factor ($γ\in(0,1)$), exact planning remains NP-hard. Second, we introduce a randomized polynomial-time approximation scheme for every fixed look-ahead depth. We then extend our approach to unknown transitions and stochastic rewards using optimism and variance-adaptive confidence bounds. The resulting algorithm achieves cumulative regret whose leading term matches classical tabular discounted RL up to logarithmic factors. Thus, although exact planning with transition look-ahead is NP-hard, efficient near-optimal planning and learning remain possible.

</details>


### [237] [Generative Marketing Mix Modeling: A Causal Inference Framework Linking GEO and GEM to Business Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11915)
*Masahiro Kato,Daiki Honma,Taka Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出生成式营销组合建模（GMMM），在无直接曝光记录下，估计GEO与GEM对企业的因果效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI改变企业触达客户的方式，但标准营销数据无法记录用户在生成式回答中看到并注意到企业名称的频率，导致GEO与GEM的效果难以测度。

Method: GMMM针对GEO，整合重复生成的答案、问题数量、跨生成系统的使用份额及注意概率；针对GEM，整合赞助投放记录与注意概率；并比较不同处理序列下的期望业务响应，给出识别因果效应的充分条件。

Result: 用英语和日语的产品推荐模拟答案，对所提方法的实证表现进行了考察。

Conclusion: GMMM为生成式引擎营销提供了可行的因果效应估计框架，弥补了生成式环境下曝光与注意数据的缺失。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence changes how firms reach customers, but standard marketing data do not record how often users see and notice a firm's name in generated answers. We develop Generative Marketing Mix Modeling (GMMM) to estimate the causal effects of Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) and Generative Engine Marketing (GEM). For GEO, GMMM combines repeated generated answers with question counts, shares of use across generative systems, and notice probabilities. For GEM, it combines records of sponsored placements with notice probabilities. GMMM compares expected business responses under alternative treatment sequences and establishes sufficient conditions for identifying the resulting effects. We investigate the empirical performance of the proposed method using simulated answers to product recommendation in English and Japanese.

</details>


### [238] [Evaluating Time-Series Foundation Models and Multimodal Dietary Context for CGM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11872)
*Bowen Zhang,Hsiu-Wen Cheng,Hongyu Yang,Evie L. Shen,Joleen Vansomphone,Yuna Li,Kerry Zhou,Zitian Qu,Suning Zhao,Xiangning Deng,Hua Zhou,Jin J. Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对8个公开CGM数据集进行系统实证研究，发现零样本基础模型不如Elastic Net/PatchTST，但轻量微调显著提升预测性能，且多模态饮食上下文（残差融合）进一步改善餐后血糖预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM可提供高频血糖动态测量并支持短期血糖预测，但时序基础模型在CGM预测中的效果及多模态饮食上下文的价值尚不明确，故需系统评估。

Method: 在统一协议下，使用8个公开CGM数据集（覆盖T1D、T2D、非糖尿病），跨多种上下文长度与预测步长，比较零样本基础模型与任务专用基线（Elastic Net、PatchTST）；对基础模型进行轻量微调；在CGMacros数据集上用基于残差的融合框架整合CGM、食物图像与宏量营养素。

Result: 零样本基础模型未能稳定超越强基线；微调Chronos-Bolt使T1D队列RMSE降低6.5%-18.4%，非糖尿病/T2D队列降低8.6%-18.2%，分布内/外均有效；残差融合使整体RMSE降低约3%、餐后RMSE降低约15%；Chronos的CGM表征与餐后血糖增量相关性高于LSTM和CatBoost。

Conclusion: 基础模型需针对CGM做专门适配才能可靠预测；饮食上下文提供CGM之外的临床上重要信号，尤其在餐后时段。

Abstract: Continuous glucose monitoring (CGM) provides high-frequency measurements of glucose dynamics and enables short-term glucose forecasting for diabetes management. Although time-series foundation models have shown strong general forecasting ability, their effectiveness for CGM prediction and the added value of multimodal dietary context remain unclear. We conduct a comprehensive empirical study using eight public CGM datasets spanning Type 1 diabetes, Type 2 diabetes, and non-diabetes populations. Under a unified protocol across multiple context lengths and prediction horizons, zero-shot foundation models did not consistently outperform strong task-specific baselines such as Elastic Net and PatchTST. In contrast, lightweight fine-tuning substantially improved forecasting performance. For example, fine-tuned Chronos-Bolt reduced RMSE by 6.5%-18.4% in the T1D cohort and by 8.6%-18.2% in the non-diabetes/T2D cohort, with comparable improvements in both in-distribution and out-of-distribution test settings. We further evaluate multimodal dietary context using CGMacros, which provides temporally aligned CGM signals, food images, and macronutrient records. A residual-based fusion framework reduced overall RMSE by approximately 3% and postprandial RMSE by approximately 15% relative to the CGM-only baseline. Moreover, Chronos-based CGM representations were more strongly correlated with observed postprandial glucose increments than representations from LSTM and CatBoost, even after those models incorporated additional dietary modalities, suggesting that pretrained temporal representations better preserve meal-induced excursion patterns. These findings show that foundation models require CGM-specific adaptation for reliable forecasting and that dietary context provides clinically meaningful signals beyond CGM alone, especially during postprandial periods.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Impedance-Based Sensitivity Analysis for Stability Enhancement of LCC-HVDC Links Connected to Weak Grids Using Grid-Forming Converters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10774)
*Luis A. Garcia-Reyes,Anup Joshi,Javier Renedo,Macarena Martin-Almenta,Oriol Gomis-Bellmunt,Eduardo Prieto-Araujo,Vinicius A. Lacerda,Xavier Guillaud*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种频域阻抗灵敏度方法，用于弱电网下LCC-HVDC的稳定性评估与增强，并结合GNC与模态阻抗分解定位交互机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱电网条件下LCC-HVDC稳定性问题突出，需要能够评估并提升稳定性的方法。

Method: 面向黑箱系统的频域辨识、广义奈奎斯特判据（GNC）多变量稳定性评估、模态阻抗分解与参与因子分析。

Result: 通过详细线性化状态空间模型与LCC-HVDC基准的非线性EMT仿真验证；改变SCR的灵敏度研究揭示孤立LCC-HVDC的稳定极限，并表明集成GFM-VSC可显著提高稳定裕度。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效评估弱电网下LCC-HVDC稳定性，GFM-VSC的集成为提升稳定性提供了有效途径。

Abstract: This paper presents a frequency-domain, impedance-based sensitivity methodology for stability assessment and enhancement of line-commutated converter HVDC (LCC-HVDC) links operating under weak-grid conditions. The methodology integrates frequency-domain identification tailored for black-box systems, the Generalized Nyquist Criterion (GNC) for multivariable stability assessment, and modal impedance decomposition with participation-factor analysis to locate and interpret interaction mechanisms. The approach is validated against a detailed linearized state-space model and nonlinear EMT simulations of an LCC-HVDC benchmark. A sensitivity study varying the grid short-circuit ratio (SCR) reveals a stability limit for the standalone LCC-HVDC link and demonstrates that the integration of a grid-forming voltage source converter (GFM-VSC) substantially increases the stability margin.

</details>


### [240] [Structural Sign Herdability in Temporal Networks: A Sufficient Condition via $π_p$-Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10785)
*Pradeep M,Twinkle Tripathy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究时变切换有向网络的群聚可控性（herdability），给出充分条件、揭示边权大小对可控矩阵符号模式的影响，并建立结构符号群聚可控性的等价图论条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时变网络被建模为固定切换序列的切换系统，其群聚可控性条件比传统切换系统更严格；且边权大小会影响可控矩阵符号模式，因此群聚可控性不仅取决于拓扑与切换时长，还取决于边权大小，需进一步刻画结构符号群聚可控性。

Method: 利用时间游走与可控矩阵元素之间的关系推导群聚可控性充分条件；引入时变子系统的并多重图与π-图概念，提出π_p-图（时变演化的π-图），将其存在性作为结构符号群聚可控性的充分条件，并建立等价图论条件。

Result: 得到时变切换有向网络群聚可控性的充分条件；证明边权大小影响可控矩阵符号模式并进而影响群聚可控性；给出结构符号群聚可控性的等价图论条件，并证明存在π_p-图可保证结构符号群聚可控性，辅以示例验证。

Conclusion: 时变网络的群聚可控性取决于网络拓扑、切换时长及边权大小；通过并多重图与π-图/π_p-图可刻画结构符号群聚可控性，π_p-图的存在性可充分保证该性质。

Abstract: In this letter, we study the herdability of temporally switching directed networks. A temporal network is modeled as a switched system with a fixed switching sequence, which imposes more restrictive herdability conditions than those of conventional switched systems. By exploiting the relationship between temporal walks and the entries of the controllability matrix, we derive sufficient conditions for herdability. We further show that the magnitude of edge weights influences the sign pattern of the controllability matrix, thereby affecting herdability. Consequently, herdability in temporal networks depends not only on the network topology and switching durations, but also on the magnitude of the edge weights.
  Motivated by this observation, we establish equivalent graph-theoretic conditions for structural sign ($\mathcal{SS}$) herdability in temporal networks. In particular, we introduce the union multigraph of temporal subsystems and propose the notion of a $π$-graph. We show that the existence of a $π_p$-graph, which is a temporally evolving $π$-graph, is sufficient to guarantee $\mathcal{SS}$ herdability. Illustrative examples are provided to demonstrate the proposed results.

</details>


### [241] [Dynamic Centrality Measures for Water Distribution Network Hydraulics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10800)
*MirSaleh Bahavarnia,Salma M. Elsherif,Ahmad F. Taha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出基于控制论的中心性指标（脆弱性向量VV），同时考虑水分配网络（WDN）的动力学与拓扑，识别最具/最不具影响力的管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WDN易受人为错误、网络攻击、网络改造等故障影响，需进行脆弱性分析。但现有图论中心性方法仅基于拓扑排序，忽略了WDN的动态特性。

Method: 将WDN建模为非线性微分代数方程（NDAE，含瞬态流DE与水量守恒AE），以管道流量为状态，在稳态流量向量附近线性化为线性常微分方程（LODE）；以管道流量为状态空间节点，提出基于节点中心性的脆弱性向量（VV）指标。

Result: 通过VV指标可识别网络中最具影响力和最不具影响力的管道，实现基于动力学与拓扑的中心性排序。

Conclusion: 该方法帮助水务工程师更好地理解WDN脆弱性，有效优先安排最具影响力管道的维护与运行工作。

Abstract: Water distribution networks (WDNs) are susceptible to various failures, including but not limited to human errors, cyber-attacks, and network modifications, necessitating the vulnerability analysis of WDNs. Graph-theoretic centrality measures---as a main class of centrality measures---aim to rank the network components solely based on their influence (i.e., criticality) on the WDN topology in the case of input changes, while overlooking the WDN dynamics. To overcome such a limitation, this paper uses a control-theoretic centrality measure to identify the network's most and least influential pipes in WDNs, by simultaneously incorporating the dynamics and topology of the WDN. First, given a WDN modeled by nonlinear differential-algebraic equations (NDAEs) consisting of transient flow dynamics as differential equation (DE) and conservation of water mass as algebraic equation (AE) and considering the pipe flow rates as states of the state-space (SS) representation, we extract a linearized system modeled by linear ordinary differential equations (LODEs) around the equilibrium (i.e., steady) flow rate vector. Second, treating pipe flow rates as SS nodes (states), we introduce a node centrality-based measure, namely \textit{vulnerability vector (VV)}, to rank the network pipes based on their influence on the dynamics and topology of the WDN in the case of input changes. In particular, the network's most and least influential pipes can be identified through such a centrality-based approach. This enables water engineers to understand the WDN's vulnerability better and effectively prioritize the maintenance and operational efforts on the most influential pipes within the WDN.

</details>


### [242] [Structured Stochastic Representations of Integrated Dynamic Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10998)
*Fredy Vides*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结构保持的"表示—辨识—决策"工作流，用四个概率局部化通过带状态索引、图约束的列随机算子刻画动态分配决策中的因果切换表示，并给出辨识性与可实施决策判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态分配决策将当前资源使用与内部状态演化、延迟回报和未来成本耦合，但既有方法难以同时保持结构、区分系数恢复与预测等价，并给出可认证的决策边界。

Method: 用四个概率局部化经regime索引的图约束列随机算子建模交互；行动前状态/情境选择局部仿射模型，动作相关变化更新后续regime，形成因果切换非线性表示；分析算子相对于图、随机约束和采样嵌入的可辨识性；以可实施的"回报—成本"可接受区域构建决策，利用有限时域误差传播给出保守分类裕度，并用同时区间区分模型相对近优与在给定有限策略类上的认证ε-最优性。

Result: 可辨识性结果将系数恢复与决策域上的预测等价分离；regime索引随机反馈在通过同一认证测试时可被接纳。合成实验（个人准备、供应商参与、客户保留）覆盖精确、算子给定和噪声反馈三种情形；多项分布实验显示随样本量增大反馈函数恢复改善、未决决策减少，但无约束离策略恢复仍受限。

Conclusion: 贡献在于提供结构保持的表示—辨识—决策工作流，而非特定领域的生理或商业标定。

Abstract: Dynamic allocation decisions couple present resource use to evolving internal conditions, delayed returns, and future costs. We represent this interaction by four probability localizations linked through regime-indexed, graph-constrained column-stochastic operators. Pre-action state or context selects a locally affine model, while action-dependent changes update subsequent regimes, yielding a causal switched representation of nonlinear evolution. We characterize operator identifiability relative to the graph, the stochastic constraints, and the sampled embedding, separating coefficient recovery from predictive equivalence on the decision domain. Decision making is then formulated through implementable return--cost acceptability regions. Finite-horizon error propagation supplies conservative classification margins, and simultaneous intervals distinguish model-relative near-optimality from certified $ε$-optimality over a declared finite policy class. Regime-indexed stochastic feedback is admitted when it satisfies the same certification test. Reproducible synthetic laboratories for personal preparation, supplier participation, and customer retention illustrate exact, operator-supplied, and noisy feedback cases. Multinomial experiments show improving recovery of the feedback function and fewer unresolved decisions with increasing sample size, while unrestricted off-policy recovery remains limited. The contribution is a structure-preserving representation--identification--decision workflow, not a domain-specific physiological or commercial calibration.

</details>


### [243] [Fusion Estimation in Multi-sensor Systems for Data Packets with Disrupted Identities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11062)
*Haoyuan Xu,Yuzhe Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 研究多传感器系统中数据包身份被打乱/错误的融合估计问题，用置换和对称群建模数据包排列，提出贝叶斯与贪心两种算法并证明其误差有界，并用EM算法处理信息稀缺场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多传感器系统中，由于设备身份分配混乱、通信协议缺陷或缺乏明确传感器标识，各传感器收到的数据包身份可能被打乱或错误，导致融合估计过程中数据分量随机洗牌，从而损害融合估计性能。

Method: 引入置换与对称群概念，将现象刻画为数据包排列问题；构造统计量以简化信息集；提出基于后验排列概率的贝叶斯融合算法，以及通过猜测可能数据排列来改善估计性能的贪心算法；并针对信息稀缺场景，采用期望最大化(EM)算法填补数据排列的先验信息。

Result: 两种算法均被证明具有期望误差界；EM算法在填补先验信息时被证明收敛正确；数值仿真验证了所提结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过置换与对称群建模数据包排列，贝叶斯与贪心算法均能实现误差有界的融合估计，EM改进方案可解决信息稀缺情形，仿真结果验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore the problem of fusion estimation for a multi-sensor system where the identity of the data packet received by each sensor may be disrupted or incorrect due to confusion in device identity allocation, communication protocol defects, or the lack of a clear sensor identifier. This can result in a random shuffle of the data components during the fusion estimation process, compromising the performance of the fusion estimation. To address this issue, we introduce the concepts of permutations and symmetry groups to describe this phenomenon as data packet permutation. We construct statistics to simplify the information set, developing two algorithms: a Bayesian approach, which performs fusion using posterior arrangement probabilities, and a greedy approach, which effectively improves estimation performance by guessing the likely data arrangement. We compare these two algorithms and demonstrate that both are expectation error-bounded. We improve algorithms for information-scarce scenarios. By employing the expectation-maximization algorithm, we fill in the prior information of data arrangement where the correct convergence is proven. Finally, we present numerical simulations to validate our results.

</details>


### [244] [Quantifying the Reality Gap for RL-Based UAV Placement at mmWave and Sub-THz](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11121)
*Abdullateef Almohamad,Mostafa Ibrahim,Sabit Ekin,Khalid Qaraqe*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文在真实城市地图上量化了毫米波/亚太赫兹UAV部署RL策略的仿真-现实差距，评估三种信道管线，揭示偏差来源并提出解决建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV在mmWave/sub-THz网络中的RL部署策略通常在简化的解析信道上训练，存在仿真-现实差距，需要量化该差距。

Method: 在多哈真实城市地图上，于{28,140,183,300}GHz载频和{50,75,100,125}m高度，对比三种信道管线：解析模型、Sionna RT全蒙特卡洛射线追踪（含ITU-R P.676-13吸收）、确定性LoS混合；用偏差、RMSE、JS散度、最优部署位移四指标刻画空间SNR分布差距。

Result: 28/140GHz约70%的-5.6/-4.8dB偏差源于蒙特卡洛欠采样，缓解后降至-1.7/-1.5dB；183GHz残留-9.2dB源于大气吸收/ITU-R P.676线型分歧；300GHz射线追踪与解析模型仅偶然一致，确定性LoS管线暴露+3.8dB结构性偏移。各载频下解析训练策略的线性域regret≥0.93。

Conclusion: 解析训练策略接近最优，但存在随载频变化的SNR偏差，需显式报告；仿真-现实差距主要由欠采样与信道模型分歧造成。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) placement in mmWave and sub-terahertz networks are typically trained on simplified analytical channels. We quantify the resulting sim-to-real gap on a real urban map of Doha, Qatar, at carriers {28, 140, 183, 300} GHz and altitudes {50, 75, 100, 125} m, evaluating three channel pipelines: an analytical model (FSPL + atmospheric absorption + cuboid LoS), full Monte-Carlo ray tracing in Sionna RT with ITU-R P.676-13 absorption, and a deterministic-LoS hybrid that reuses Sionna's mesh under a closed-form path-gain expression. We formalize the gap on the spatial SNR distribution via four metrics, namely bias, RMSE, Jensen-Shannon divergence, and optimum-deployment displacement. Three findings emerge: at 28/140 GHz, $\sim$70% of the apparent -5.6/-4.8 dB Sionna bias is Monte-Carlo undersampling and shrinks to -1.7/-1.5 dB after mitigation; at 183 GHz a -9.2 dB residual isolates the atmospheric absorption / ITU-R P.676 line-shape disagreement; at 300 GHz the stochastic ray tracer agrees with the analytical model only coincidentally, with a +3.8 dB structural offset exposed by the deterministic-LoS pipeline. Across all carriers the linear-domain regret of the analytical-trained policy stays $\geq$ 0.93, indicating practical near-optimality but with a carrier-resolved SNR bias that warrants explicit reporting.

</details>


### [245] [Exact PID and PI Gain Regions for Uncertain Non-Affine MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11130)
*Cheng Zhao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文针对非仿射MIMO系统，精确刻画了保证一致指数调节的PID/PI增益区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结果多基于特定Lyapunov构造给出充分条件，无法刻画保证一致指数调节的全部PID增益。

Method: 对二阶系统，分别刻画共同二次内区域与基于线性子类的外区域；对一阶系统，利用不确定性边界上的无损线性约化与共同二次证书。

Result: 二阶系统中两区域重合，精确刻画PID增益区域；一阶系统得到精确PI区域。两区域均严格大于已有充分区域。

Conclusion: 提出端点重合与边界族等价两条精确增益区域刻画路径，并指出非线性不确定性类可刻画性的开放问题。

Abstract: Characterizing all PID gains that guarantee uniform exponential regulation of uncertain nonlinear systems remains a fundamental problem. Most existing results provide only sufficient conditions derived from a particular Lyapunov construction. This paper gives exact gain regions, defined directly by uniform regulation over the entire uncertainty class, for two classes of nonaffine MIMO systems with $m\ge2$ controlled coordinates and input channels. For systems of second order under PID control, we independently characterize a common quadratic inner region and an outer region based on a linear subclass. The two regions coincide and therefore characterize the PID gain region exactly at the system level. For systems of first order under PI control, a lossless reduction to a linear family on the uncertainty boundary, combined with a common quadratic certificate, yields the exact PI region. Both regions strictly enlarge existing sufficient regions, and the PID result also provides a sequential tuning rule. A compact linear family with one uncertain parameter shows that common quadratic certification is not generally lossless. These results establish endpoint coincidence and boundary family equivalence as two routes to exact gain regions and motivate the broader question of which nonlinear uncertainty classes admit such characterizations.

</details>


### [246] [A Counterexample to Two Representative Unit Aggregation Formulations for Unit Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11139)
*Zixuan Duan,Zhengshuo Li*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该文指出两种常用的慢爬坡机组聚合公式（PCUC与TUA）并非可行域精确，无法保证聚合轨迹可分解为可行的机组级方案，并会高估爬坡灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机组聚合可消除相同机组间的对称性，但只有每个聚合轨迹都能对应可行的机组级实现时，聚合公式才是可行域精确的。作者要检验常用的慢爬坡机组聚合公式是否满足这一要求。

Method: 针对慢爬坡机组的 p-clustered unit commitment（PCUC）和 tight unit aggregation（TUA），构造一个满足两者所有聚合约束、却不存在可行分解的反例；并在文献已报道的复现算例上进行实验验证。

Result: 反例表明，PCUC与TUA均无法保证机组级出力分配的时间一致性；实验进一步验证，这种不可分解的不可行性甚至会出现在聚合模型的最优解中。

Conclusion: PCUC与TUA仍可能高估爬坡灵活性，因此两者并非可行域精确的聚合模型；为相同慢爬坡机组开发可高效求解的精确聚合模型仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: Unit aggregation removes symmetry among identical generators in unit commitment, but an aggregate formulation is feasible-region exact only if every aggregate trajectory it admits has a feasible unit-level realization. This letter shows that two commonly used aggregation formulations for slow-ramping units, i.e., p-clustered unit commitment (PCUC) and tight unit aggregation (TUA), cannot satisfy this requirement. We construct a counterexample that satisfies all aggregate constraints of both formulations yet admits no feasible disaggregation. This counterexample reveals a limitation common to both formulations: they do not guarantee intertemporal consistency of unit-level output allocations. Experiments on the replication cases reported in published literature further verify that such infeasibility of disaggregation can even occur in optimal solutions of aggregate models. This letter demonstrates that PCUC and TUA can still overestimate ramping flexibility. Developing exact aggregation models that can be solved efficiently for identical slow-ramping units remains a challenge.

</details>


### [247] [Quantifying the Unintentional Islanding Risk: A Comparative Study on Active Distribution Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11400)
*Harshit Nayak,Edoardo Daccò,Jose Luis Rueda Torres,Davide Falabretti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 逆变器型电源(IBR)占比上升改变主动配电网运行。本文用EMT模型研究MV配电网并网转孤岛动态,发现同步发电机(SG)调节模式与惯量决定孤岛能否持续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IBR增长对电网稳定、保护与可靠性带来担忧,需明确配电网并网转孤岛过程中非计划孤岛能否被保护检测,以及SG调节方式与惯量的影响。

Method: 在DIgSILENT PowerFactory中搭建GFL变流器与SG的详细电磁暂态(EMT)模型,针对中压ADN并网转孤岛,对比两种SG调节模式:固定设定值不调节(现状)与带调速器/AVR的主动频率电压调节。

Result: 固定功率设定值时,孤立网络数十秒内漂移出保护阈值,持续未检测孤岛风险有限;启用SG频率电压调节时,无需考虑系统惯量,足以无限期维持非计划孤岛且不触发常规保护。

Conclusion: SG的主动频率/电压调节比惯量更能决定非计划孤岛的持续性,传统保护难以检测此类孤岛,对ADN保护策略提出新挑战。

Abstract: The growing share of Inverter-Based Resources (IBRs) is changing the operation of modern Active Distribution Networks (ADNs), raising concerns for grid stability, protection, and reliability. In this paper, we investigate the dynamic behavior of a Medium-Voltage (MV) portion of an ADN during the transition from grid-connected to islanded operation. The study considers a Grid-Following (GFL) converter and Synchronous Generators (SGs) operated under two distinct regulation modes: a fixed-setpoint, non-regulating condition representative of present-day distribution networks, and an active frequency/voltage-regulating condition representative of SGs equipped with governor and AVR control. Detailed electromagnetic transient (EMT) models of both technologies have been developed in DIgSILENT PowerFactory that quantifies the sensitivity of island persistence to SG regulation mode and inertia. The results show that when DERs operate at a fixed power setpoint, the risk of forming a sustained, undetected electrical island is limited, since the isolated network drifts out of the protection thresholds within tens of seconds. Conversely, enabling frequency and voltage regulation on the SGs is sufficient to sustain an unintentional island indefinitely without triggering conventional protection, regardless of system inertia.

</details>


### [248] [A Primal-Dual Formulation for Pricing Static Voltage Stability Services within a Unit Commitment Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11436)
*Peng Wang,Luis Badesa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 针对弱母线静态电压稳定服务的定价问题，本文提出基于原-对偶（primal-dual）公式的定价方法，可稳定生成收益充足的影子价格，使所有参与单元回收成本，无需额外上调支付。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高比例IBR电力系统中，多数变流器运行于GFL模式；受网络拓扑影响，部分母线天然具有低短路比（SCR）。GFL-IBR接入此类弱母线需关注静态电压稳定。已有市场机制（如承诺同步发电机以降低低SCR母线等效阻抗、GFL-IBR自适应无功支撑）可激励电压稳定服务，但既有影子价格计算方法（如"restricted""dispatchable"方法）仅以社会剩余最大化为目标，忽略机组盈利性，无法保证电压稳定服务提供者的运行成本回收，因此需要新的定价方法。

Method: 本文提出一种基于原-对偶公式（primal-dual formulation）的定价方法，用于计算电压稳定服务的影子价格，以在定价中纳入成本回收要求。

Result: 案例研究表明，所提方法能够持续产生收益充足的影子价格（revenue-adequate shadow prices），使所有参与单元回收其成本，且无需补充性上调支付（uplift payments）。

Conclusion: 所提原-对偶定价方法可有效满足电压稳定服务提供者的成本回收要求，弥补既有方法在盈利性方面的不足。

Abstract: In modern power systems with high penetration of Inverter-Based Resources (IBR), most converters operate in Grid-Following (GFL) mode. Some buses exhibit inherently low Short-Circuit Ratios (SCRs), a property majorly shaped by network topology. The integration of GFL-IBR onto such weak buses thus demands attention to static voltage stability. To address this issue, market mechanisms have been proposed to incentivize generators to provide voltage stability services, such as commitment of synchronous generators for reducing the equivalent impedance at low SCR buses and adaptive reactive power support from GFL-IBR. To compute shadow prices for these services, previously proposed methods such as the `restricted' and `dispatchable' approaches may fail to guarantee operating cost recovery for voltage-stability service providers. As the resulting prices are determined purely from a social surplus maximization objective, the profitability of units is entirely overlooked. This suggests that new pricing methodologies are needed to satisfy cost-recovery requirements. Therefore, this paper proposes a pricing method based on a primal-dual formulation. Case studies demonstrate that the proposed method can consistently produce revenue-adequate shadow prices, enabling all participating units to recover their costs without supplementary uplift payments.

</details>


### [249] [Critic-Free Policy Iteration for Continuous-Time Zero-Sum Games: A Policy-Space Riccati Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11564)
*Jiacheng Wu,Yang Zhu,Hongye Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出无评论家(critic-free)的策略迭代方法求解连续时间线性零和博弈，直接在联合策略空间刻画鞍点策略，引入策略博弈Riccati方程(PGRE)，并给出数据驱动版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将二次值矩阵作为迭代变量，需要估计critic(值函数)，存在可辨识性问题且计算与内存开销大。作者希望绕过值矩阵，直接在策略(actor)空间进行迭代，从而避免critic辨识需求。

Method: 1) 在联合策略空间直接刻画鞍点策略；2) 引入PGRE，其未知量仅为策略增益，证明其解与博弈代数Riccati方程的对称解一一对应；3) 基于稳定锚点直接在actor空间做PI，证明actor空间Jacobian在每个稳定策略处非奇异，且策略序列与同时PI一致；4) 未知动态下，利用单批次数据和端点增量的零空间投影消除值矩阵，得到仅含actor的回归；5) 建立唯一策略恢复的充要秩条件，并证明其迭代不变性。

Result: 证明critic可辨识性并非必要；充要秩条件迭代不变。电力系统频率调节例子验证了收敛性与策略恢复；可扩展性测试显示计算与内存需求大幅降低。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需critic的连续时间线性零和博弈策略迭代框架，在理论与数据驱动实现上均可行，收敛性有保证，并显著降低计算与内存成本。

Abstract: This paper develops a critic-free policy iteration (PI) method for continuous-time linear zero-sum games. The central idea is to characterize the saddle-point policies directly in the joint policy space, rather than treating the quadratic value matrix as an iterative variable. A policy game Riccati equation (PGRE) is introduced whose unknowns are policy gains only. Its solutions are shown to be in one-to-one correspondence with the symmetric solutions of the game algebraic Riccati equation. Based on a stabilizing anchor, PI is performed directly in the actor space. The actor-space Jacobian is nonsingular at every stabilizing policy, and the resulting policy sequence coincides with that of simultaneous PI. For unknown dynamics, a data-driven algorithm uses a single batch of data and nullspace projection of endpoint increments to eliminate the value matrix, yielding an actor-only regression. A necessary and sufficient rank condition for unique policy recovery is established and shown to be iteration-invariant, demonstrating that critic identifiability is unnecessary. A power systems frequency-regulation example verifies convergence and policy recovery, while scalability tests demonstrate substantial reductions in computational and memory requirements.

</details>


### [250] [Elementwise Positivity of the Solution to Lyapunov Equation for Hurwitz Companion Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11651)
*Miaomiao Wang,Patrizio Colaneri,Jie Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 借助AI证明：对任意实对称半正定强制矩阵Q，当状态矩阵为实Hurwitz伴随矩阵时，连续时间Lyapunov方程的唯一解逐元素非负，印证了先前的猜想；但离散时间情形不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证关于Lyapunov方程解非负性的一个先前猜想，明确连续时间与离散时间情形的差异。

Method: 借助AI协助证明；引入Horner多项式矩阵工具；证明不依赖状态矩阵的谱假设。

Result: 证明了连续时间猜想成立（Hurwitz伴随矩阵解非负）；给出反例表明离散时间对应猜想为假，Schur伴随矩阵即使强制为单位阵也会产生含负非对角元的解。

Conclusion: 连续时间Lyapunov方程的非负性猜想成立，而离散时间版本被反例推翻，凸显两类系统本质差异。

Abstract: We prove, with the aid of AI, that for every real symmetric forcing matrix $Q\succeq0$, the unique solution of a continuous-time Lyapunov equation is entrywise nonnegative whenever the state matrix is a real Hurwitz companion matrix. This proves an earlier conjecture. The proof makes no assumption on the spectrum of the state matrix, and is made possible by means of using Horner polynomial matrices. We also show that discrete-time counterpart of the conjecture is false: Counterexamples can be readily constructed to show that Schur companion matrices, including one generated by a polynomial with all positive coefficients, yield solutions with negative off-diagonal entries even with identity forcing.

</details>


### [251] [Mixed-integer optimization for multi-year military aircraft fleet management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11710)
*Matteo Vescovi,Raffaele Giuseppe Cestari,Roberto Valdambrini,Andrea Mercurio,Valentina Breschi,Mara Tanelli*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文提出面向国防领域的多年期飞行与维修规划策略，处理非理想初始机队状态与复杂检查方案，通过多目标优化最大化机队长期可用性，并引入滚动时域、贝叶斯优化调参和飞机权重方案提升实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有飞行与维修规划策略多假设理想条件，而实际规划常涉及非额定初始机队状态和复杂检查方案，因此需要更贴近实际的多年期规划方法。

Method: 构建多目标优化模型，刻画飞机使用与维修间的紧耦合，同时满足循环检查要求和有限维修坞容量；并引入三种互补策略：滚动时域方法降低计算负担、贝叶斯优化自动调整代价函数系数、基于机队状态统计的飞机特定权重方案。

Result: 在三个真实基准场景上的结果验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该多年期多目标规划策略结合多种增强机制，可有效应对实际飞行与维修规划挑战并提升机队长期可用性。

Abstract: While existing strategies for Flight and Maintenance Planning for the defense sector generally address idealized conditions, real-world planning often involve non-nominal initial fleet states and complex inspection schemes. To address these challenges, we propose a multi-year planning strategy that maximizes long-term fleet availability. The formulation incorporates multiple competing objectives capturing the tight coupling between aircraft usage and maintenance, while enforcing cyclic inspection requirements and limited maintenance-dock capacity. To enhance practical applicability, we introduce three complementary strategies: a receding-horizon approach to reduce computational burden, a Bayesian Optimization routine to automatically tune cost-function coefficients, and an aircraft-specific weighting scheme that adapts model priorities based on fleet-condition statistics. Results on three realistic benchmark scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of the approach.

</details>


### [252] [Construction of Control Lyapunov-Barrier Functions from CLF-CBF Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11746)
*Bo Wang,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究如何从给定的CLF和CBF构造控制Lyapunov-barrier函数(CLBF)，提出基于联合水平集可容许性的构造方法，并通过一阶PDE给出乘法型和幂型显式族，同时揭示可积性障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制Lyapunov函数(CLF)与控制障碍函数(CBF)分别保证稳定性与安全性，但二者结合为CLBF时构造困难，且基于原始CLF的反馈可能违反安全约束，因此需要系统性的CLBF构造理论。

Method: 考虑形如W=F(V,h)的复合函数（随CLF值增大、不随障碍值增大），证明CLBF下降条件可由一个非负标量权重完全刻画；该权重仅依赖(V,h)，故导出联合水平集可容许条件；进而通过一阶PDE从可容许权重构造CLBF，给出乘法型与幂型显式族。

Result: 得到CLBF下降条件的完整刻画与联合水平集可容许性判据；构造出显式的乘法型和幂型CLBF族；发现可积性障碍，表明仅有可容许性不足以保证properness；两个非线性例子验证构造并实现原CLF反馈失效情形下的安全镇定。

Conclusion: CLBF的构造可归结为联合水平集上的可容许权重选取及相应PDE求解，但需额外条件保证properness；该方法能在保持安全的前提下实现镇定。

Abstract: This paper studies the construction of control Lyapunov-barrier functions (CLBFs) from a given control Lyapunov function (CLF) $V$ and control barrier function (CBF) $h$. We consider functions of the form $W=F(V,h)$ that increase with the CLF value and do not increase with the barrier value, and show that the CLBF decrease condition is completely characterized by a nonnegative scalar weight that governs the relative contributions of the CLF and CBF. Because this weight depends only on $(V,h)$, the same value must satisfy the decrease condition at all states sharing the same CLF and CBF values, which leads to a joint-level-set admissibility condition. We then construct CLBFs from admissible weights through a first-order partial differential equation (PDE) and obtain explicit multiplicative and power-type families as special cases. We further identify an integrability obstruction showing that admissibility alone does not guarantee properness. Two nonlinear examples illustrate the constructions and demonstrate safe stabilization in cases where feedback based on the original CLF violates the safety constraint.

</details>


### [253] [Predefined-Time Leaderless Consensus Under Denial-of-Service Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11781)
*Lohitvel Gopikannan,Shashi Ranjan Kumar,Abhinav Sinha*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究DoS攻击下无领导者二阶非线性多智能体系统的预定义时间弹性一致性，提出切换滑模协议，在攻击间隔利用相对信息、通信中断时利用局部速度反馈，通过Lyapunov分析给出增益条件，实现与初值无关的预定义收敛时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键应用中的协调需求要求多智能体系统在DoS攻击导致通信链路中断时仍能实现一致性，且收敛时间需可预设且不依赖初始条件。

Method: 针对受有界扰动、强连通有向图（攻击时链路同时失效）的系统，设计切换滑模协议：无攻击时用相对位置与速度信息，通信中断时用局部速度反馈；时间缩放函数在每次中断时保持常数、恢复通信后继续演化，以计入可用一致时间。在攻击时长和频率有界的条件下，通过Lyapunov分析推导充分增益条件。

Result: 尽管存在有界扰动，智能体仍能在实际收敛时间（预设收敛时长加上截至该时刻的累计攻击时长）内实现位置与速度一致性；预设收敛时长与初始条件无关，无攻击时实际收敛时间退化为该预设时长。

Conclusion: 所提切换滑模协议在DoS攻击下实现了预定义时间弹性一致性，攻击导致的收敛延迟可量化为累计攻击时长，且收敛时间不依赖初始条件。

Abstract: This paper addresses predefined-time resilient consensus of leaderless second-order nonlinear multi-agent systems under denial-of-service (DoS) attacks, motivated by coordination requirements in safety-critical applications. The agents are subject to bounded external disturbances and communicate over a strongly connected directed graph whose links are simultaneously disabled during attacks. We develop a switching sliding-mode protocol with the objective of reaching an invariant manifold of position and velocity agreement. The protocol uses relative position and velocity information during attack-free intervals and local velocity feedback during communication blackouts. A time-scaling function remains constant during each blackout and resumes evolving when communication is restored, accounting for the time available for consensus. Under bounds on attack duration and frequency, we derive sufficient gain conditions through a Lyapunov analysis. We show that, despite bounded disturbances, the agents achieve position and velocity consensus by a realistic settling time equal to a prescribed convergence duration plus the cumulative attack duration up to the realistic settling time. The prescribed convergence duration is independent of the initial conditions, and the realistic settling time reduces to that duration in the absence of attacks.

</details>


### [254] [Predefined-Time Resilient Integral Reinforcement Learning for Input-Constrained Unknown Nonlinear Systems Under FDI Attacks and Disturbances: A Fully Data-Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11815)
*Tien Dat Vu,Minh Doan*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种积分强化学习框架，用于未知动态、输入受限、存在扰动与对抗信号的非线性系统最优控制，可在设计阶段预设收敛时间并保证执行器约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性系统常面临动态未知、执行器受限、扰动与对抗信号等问题，且现有学习方法难以让设计者事先指定收敛时间。

Method: 构建积分强化学习框架，无需精确系统动态知识；利用当前与历史记录数据训练critic，无需持续激励；学习增益由预设收敛期限直接确定；通过Lyapunov分析证明状态-critic耦合系统的实用预定义时间收敛。

Result: 在扰动与对抗信道存在下，实现了状态-critic耦合系统的实用预定义时间收敛，控制器始终满足执行器约束；并通过双连杆机械臂稳定控制验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为受约束、受扰动的未知非线性系统提供了一种可预设收敛时间的学习型最优控制方案，仿真验证了其可行性。

Abstract: This paper investigates optimal control for nonlinear systems with unknown dynamics, input constraints, disturbances, and adversarial signals. The objective is to develop a learning-based control method that allows the designer to prescribe the desired convergence time in advance. An integral reinforcement-learning framework is proposed to avoid requiring exact knowledge of the system dynamics while ensuring that the control input always satisfies the actuator constraints. Current and recorded data are combined to train the critic without requiring persistent excitation. The learning gain is selected directly from the prescribed convergence deadline. Lyapunov analysis is then used to establish practical predefined-time convergence of the coupled state-critic system in the presence of disturbances and adversarial channels. The effectiveness of the proposed method is validated through the stabilization control of a two-link robot manipulator.

</details>


### [255] [Acoustic-based Guidance for Automatic Docking of Holonomic AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11821)
*Ravi Regalo,David Cabecinhas,António Pascoal*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全声学传感架构，使AUV在无精确对接站位置姿态信息且存在未知洋流的条件下自动完成对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低能见度环境中相机失效，且对接站位置姿态未知、洋流未知，传统对接方法难以可靠工作，因此需要基于声学的自动对接方案。

Method: 采用一对超短基线（USBL）传感器，分别搭载于AUV和海底对接站；用SO(3)上的非线性互补滤波器估计相对姿态，用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计相对位置，并将位姿估计输入SE(3)上的几何控制器执行对接机动；系统在专用软件套件中实现。

Result: 通过仿真和水池/水域试验进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该全声学对接系统能够在缺乏精确先验信息与存在未知洋流的情况下完成AUV自动对接。

Abstract: This paper describes a system to automatically dock an AUV onto a docking station without precise knowledge of the position and orientation of the latter, in the presence of unknown ocean currents, using a fully acoustic sensing architecture. The system relies on a pair of Ultrashort Baseline sensors, one onboard the vehicle and one installed on a seabed-resident docking station, enabling operation in low-visibility environments where cameras are ineffective. Relative orientation is estimated by a nonlinear complementary filter on $SO(3)$, while an Extended Kalman Filter provides relative position, supplying pose estimates to a geometric controller on $SE(3)$ that executes the docking manoeuvre. The complete system is implemented in a dedicated software suite and validated in simulation and water trials.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [256] [EVTradeMatch: A Mobility-Aware Multi-Objective Matching Framework for EV--EV Energy Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10551)
*Md. Mahfujur Rahman,Alistair Barros,Raja Jurdak,Darshika Koggalahewa*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出 EVTradeMatch，一个预测引导的多目标优化框架，用于移动感知的 EV-EV 能量交易，通过 NSGA-II 在广泛动态场景下逼近帕累托高效解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在充电基础设施有限的情况下，EV 间的点对点能量交易可提升充电灵活性，但有效的 EV-EV 交易需在旅途特定条件下协调供需匹配。

Method: 基于 EVNextTrade 学习排序模型定义充电节点适宜度分数（预测引导信号与优化目标），将匹配问题建模为多目标混合整数线性规划，最大化匹配覆盖率、转移能量和节点适宜度，同时最小化移动成本；并设计定制化 NSGA-II 求解帕累托前沿。

Result: 相比基于邻近和拍卖的 SOTA 方法，转移能量提升 74.2–82.8%，节点适宜度提升 8.3–84.4%，匹配覆盖率提升 3.74–25.07 个百分点；均衡解达 53.07±0.76% 覆盖率、转移 1701.94±17.03 kWh，但移动成本较高。

Conclusion: EVTradeMatch 支持根据运营优先级在高覆盖、高能量、低移动成本和高适宜度解之间灵活选择，帕累托前沿分析验证了其灵活性。

Abstract: Peer-to-peer energy trading among electric vehicles (EVs) can improve charging flexibility under limited charging infrastructure, but effective EV--EV trading requires coordinated provider--consumer matching under journey-specific conditions. This paper proposes EVTradeMatch, a prediction-guided multi-objective optimization framework for mobility-aware EV--EV energy trading. Building on the EVNextTrade study, a prior learning-to-rank model for charging-node recommendation, we define charging-node suitability as a prediction-derived score reflecting the appropriateness of assigning a provider--consumer pair to a candidate charging node based on mobility, energy, and contextual trading features. This score is used as a guidance signal and as an explicit optimization objective rather than as a hard selection rule. The EV--EV matching problem is formulated as a multi-objective mixed-integer linear program that maximizes matching coverage, transferred energy, and charging-node suitability while minimizing mobility cost under spatial, temporal, one-to-one matching, and charging-node capacity constraints. To approximate Pareto-efficient solutions in wide-area dynamic settings, we develop a tailored non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II). Experimental results show that EVTradeMatch improves transferred energy by 74.2--82.8% and charging-node suitability by 8.3--84.4% compared with proximity- and auction-based state-of-the-art methods, while improving matching coverage by 3.74--25.07 percentage points. Balanced NSGA-II solutions achieve 53.07$\pm$0.76% matching coverage and transfer 1701.94$\pm$17.03 kWh, with higher mobility cost as an explicit trade-off against travel-minimizing methods.
